## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route for a read-and-discuss investor audience: disclaimer, presenters, contents, company story, financials, transaction, then appendix, with divider pages (5, 8, 29, 39) marking each section. Most main-body slides pair a headline claim with a supporting chart, as on slides 15, 24, 28 and 33, and the template, palette and footer are consistent. The weaknesses are real, though: slide 43's waterfall is hard to follow, with a mislabeled step order and a callout bracket that is unclear, slides 41, 42 and 47 to 50 are dense unreadable tables, and the disclaimer pages 2 and 3 are walls of tiny text. Footnotes are very small, and several slides, such as 10, 11 and 36, are plain lists or tables with little visual emphasis.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the claim-style headlines with a supporting chart (slides 15, 24, 28), the consistent teal palette and footer, the section divider pages, and the large-number KPI tiles on slides 11, 16 and 18.

**Most useful repair:** Simplify the slide 43 waterfall with clearer step order and labeling, and cut the dense financial tables on slides 41, 42 and 47 to 50 to readable size or split them across pages.

**What would change the score:** I would shorten the logo-heavy and text-heavy pages (6, 9, 10) and pull the key capital structure rows on slide 36 into a clearer hierarchy.

_Confidence high. 50 of 50 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a legal disclaimer and forward-looking statements covering the proposed business combination between WeWork and BowX Acquisition Corp., followed by presenter introductions and a contents page. It then moves into a section on WeWork and the future of work, covering the leadership team, company values, global network and scale, the platform and its growth drivers, the path to recovery after 2020 cost cuts, membership mix and enterprise customers, and offerings such as All Access, Marketplace and Platform. A financial and transaction section follows, with revenue and Adj. EBITDA projections through 2024, revenue by geography and product, unit economics for mature locations, liquidity and cash flow, the capital structure, transaction terms, pro forma valuation, sources and uses, and board composition. An appendix defines the key terms and metrics. The deck closes on its financial and transaction summary and definitions. Several slides pair a single headline claim with a chart or table, which makes the logic easy to follow.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the WeWork deck have?**

The WeWork deck has 50 pages. Its contents page on slide 5 lists four sections: Introduction, WeWork & the Future of Work, Financial & Transaction Overview, and Appendix.

**What revenue does the WeWork deck show for 2021?**

Slide 11 lists $3B+ as 2021E Revenue, and slide 15 shows Total Revenue of $3.2 for 2019A, 2020A and 2021E ($ in billions). Slide 11 notes that the 2021E revenue projection includes a 4% IndiaCo management fee and excludes ChinaCo financials.

**What cost savings does the WeWork deck report for 2020?**

Slide 16 reports about $1.1B in Functional Expense Savings, about $400M in OpEx Savings, and $200M+ in savings from 200+ lease exits and amendments. The slide attributes the OpEx savings mainly to reduced foot traffic, restructuring of the community team and ongoing rent renegotiations in 2020.

**Does the WeWork deck say how the company's memberships are split by customer size?**

Slide 11 states that 50%+ of total memberships are Enterprise. Slide 17 shows the split across Small & Medium Businesses (1-99 employees), Medium & Large Businesses (100-499 employees) and Enterprise (500+ employees).

Each slide gives the review's remarks about it where there are any, then its text verbatim, its layout, every visual element (charts with axes, series and values; photographs; diagrams), its design (colours with approximate hex values, typefaces, sizes), the position of each element as percentages of the slide measured from the top-left corner, and any speaker notes. Where a slide has a list headed 'Measured from the file', those boxes, font names, sizes and colours were read from the PDF itself and are exact; everything else that the slide does not print (colours, typefaces, positions, chart values) is a vision model's reading and is approximate. Subject: four dark blue upholstered lounge armchairs with blue cushions arranged around a light oak rectangular coffee table on a tan woven sisal rug. Behind are a long oak dining table with cane-seated chairs, tall arched windows with white frames, a potted banana-leaf plant on the right and a bar counter with black stools on the left. ### Slide 4

**Text**

Today’s presenters

Chairman and Co-CEO

wework
© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Single white slide with a title top-left at about 2.7% from the left and 6% from the top. Below it, two group headings (WeWork over the first two columns, BowX Acquisition Corp. over the last two) each sit above a thin teal rule. Four equal portraits in circles sit in a row beneath, each with a bold teal name and a grey role line centred underneath. The footer at bottom right holds the page number, a separator bar, the wework wordmark and the copyright line, all right-aligned to about 97.5% of the width.

**Visuals**

Four black-and-white circular portrait photographs, each head-and-shoulders, framed in a teal ring. (1) Sandeep Mathrani: smiling man with greying hair in a dark zip-up sweater over a checked collared shirt, light grey backdrop, slightly off-centre, full circle crop. (2) Ben Dunham: smiling man in a dark blazer over a checked shirt, dark background with out-of-focus shelving, centred, full circle crop. (3) Vivek Ranadivé: man with a serious expression and slight smile in a dark suit, white shirt and grey patterned tie, light grey backdrop, centred, full circle crop. (4) Murray Rode: broadly smiling bald man with rimless-top dark glasses, open white collar and grey tweed jacket, very light grey backdrop, centred, full circle crop. Photos are neutral greyscale with soft, even studio lighting; no overlays or tints. Two thin horizontal teal rules sit under the group headings, one spanning about 5% to 46% of the width and one about 54% to 95%. The wework wordmark is a small black serif logo in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradients or textures. Title is dark charcoal #222222 in a geometric humanist sans-serif (Gill Sans-like), medium weight, roughly 5.4% of slide height. Group headings are dark charcoal #222222, same family, medium weight, about 4.2% of slide height. Names are bold teal #0A8BB0 at about 4.4% of slide height. Role lines are regular dark grey #333333 at about 2.7% of slide height. Accent colour is teal #0A8BB0 for names, heading rules and portrait rings. Rings are about 0.4% of slide height in stroke width. Shapes are plain circles with no shadows and no corner radii. Footer page number and separator are grey #666666; copyright is grey #999999 in a small bold sans-serif at about 1.2% of slide height; the wordmark is black.

### Slide 5

**Text**

Contents
01 Introduction
02 WeWork & the Future of Work
03 Financial & Transaction Overview
04 Appendix
4
|
wework
© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Two-column split: a full-bleed photograph fills the left 38.1% of the slide height-to-edge, and the white right area holds a left-aligned title 'Contents' at about x 50% with a numbered contents list beneath it. The page number and wework logo sit bottom right, with the copyright line below them, all aligned to a right margin of about 2.5%.

**Visuals**

Photograph, full-bleed, left edge to top and bottom edges, 38.1% of slide width. Subject: the reception area of a WeWork office, with a burl-wood reception desk with a dark marble top in the lower left, a woman with glasses and a purple top leaning on the desk, and a woman in a cream blazer and pink-red pencil skirt and black heels at a check-in tablet on a stand. Stacked books sit on the desk at left and a small bouquet of pink flowers sits on the counter. Behind is a wall of vertical wood panelling, a large abstract multicolour painting (pink, blue, teal, yellow and black shapes) and a glass-walled room with a turnstile at the right. A long linear pendant light runs diagonally across the upper half, with several small white ceiling spotlights and two black pendant cords. Composition: medium-wide shot, camera at standing height, subjects in the lower-middle. Lighting: soft, warm interior light with bright white glow from the linear light. Dominant colours: warm wood browns and ochre, pink and blue of the painting, grey stone floor, cream white. Not cropped, not boxed, no overlay or tint. No other graphics on the slide apart from the wework logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with the photograph covering the left 38.1% of the slide. White background. Title 'Contents' in near-black #111111 sans-serif (Karla-like geometric grotesque), regular to medium weight, about 5.7% of slide height. Active list item 'Introduction' in teal #3FA7C3, medium weight, about 4.4% of slide height; inactive items in light grey #BBBBBB, medium weight, same size. Numerals 01 to 04 in a lighter weight, about 3.0% of slide height, same colour as their item. Footer page number in dark grey #333333. Copyright line in grey #999999, bold, about 1.23% of slide height. Logo wordmark 'wework' in black #111111 high-contrast serif, bold. No shapes with corner radii, no shadows, no gradients. A teal #3FA7C3 rectangle covering the left 26.4% of the slide sits behind the photograph and is hidden by it. Text sits flush left with no bullets.

**Layout**

Three columns of content in a two-row grid, framed by a title at top left and a subtitle line beneath it that spans nearly the full width. The centre column holds a circular portrait with a name box directly beneath it, and the lower row has section headings with logos. The page number, wework logo and copyright line sit in the bottom-right corner, with a left margin of about 2.7% and a right margin of about 2.6%.

**Visuals**

Circular black-and-white portrait photograph of a middle-aged South Asian man in a dark suit, white shirt and patterned tie, head and shoulders, facing the camera with a slight smile, cropped to a circle with a thin teal outline (#1A8CA8, about 0.3% of slide height). The photo is set against a light grey studio backdrop and is lit softly from the front-left. Teal line icons (about 1.4% stroke weight, colour #1A8CA8): an atom-like orbit symbol above Tech Visionary, a circuit-board cube above Using Technology to Disrupt Industries, a gear with squares and arcs above Company Builder, a basketball above Sports, Media, and Real Estate Operator, and a handshake above Strong Investment Track Record. Each icon sits above a thin horizontal teal rule. Logos: Intel (black), Goldman Sachs (black serif), J.P.Morgan (black serif), Citi (navy with red arc), amazon (black with orange smile), YAHOO! (purple), eBay (multicolour), FedEx (purple and orange), Delta (red triangle with navy wordmark), bowX (black with magenta X), nielsen (teal serif with dotted underline), TIBCO (teal), Teknekron (grey), webex (green with blue), Google (multicolour G), Golden State Warriors (blue and gold), Sacramento Kings (purple with black). The centre name box is a pale teal rectangle with a teal border.

### Slide 7

**Text**

Compelling opportunity at the intersection of value, growth & aspiration
Value
wework bowx
Growth
Aspiration
Why WeWork?
New leadership brings vision & transparency
Large addressable market
Significant competitive moat
Proven business model through downturn
Multiple vectors for growth
Clear path to profitability
6
wework
© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white slide: a three-circle Venn diagram occupies the left half (about x 7 to 55%, y 16 to 88%) and a seven-row text list occupies the right column (x 59.5 to 91.6%, y 20 to 88%). The title sits top-left in one line at x 2.7%, y 6.1%, spanning nearly the full width with left alignment; the footer carries the page number 6, a vertical divider and the wework wordmark at bottom right, with the copyright line beneath, all right-aligned to about 97.5% of width.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a three-circle Venn with translucent outlined circles. Top circle labelled Value, bottom-left circle labelled Growth, bottom-right circle labelled Aspiration. A white centre circle sits over the intersection of all three and carries the logo lockup 'wework' over 'bowX'. Circle fills are semi-transparent (about 30% opacity): Value is light cyan, Growth is pale grey, Aspiration is pale aqua; where circles overlap the tints combine. Each circle has a thin teal outline (about 1 px, #1AA0B0). No arrows or connectors. Logos: the central 'wework bowX' lockup (wework wordmark in a heavy dark serif, bowX in a sans-serif with a red-pink and blue X mark) and the small wework wordmark in the footer. Right column: a list of six statements separated by thin light-grey rules, preceded by the heading 'Why WeWork?'. No chart or table.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White #FFFFFF background, flat, no gradients or shadows. Title text near-black #000000 in a geometric grotesque sans-serif (Founders Grotesk style), regular weight, sentence case. Heading 'Why WeWork?' in teal #3AAFC4, medium weight. Body list in dark charcoal #2B2D33, bold/semibold grotesque sans-serif. Diagram labels Value, Growth and Aspiration in dark teal #0C4F57, medium weight. Accents: teal #1AA0B0 circle outlines and rules, pink-red #E8175D and blue #3FB0F0 in the bowX mark. Title cap height about 4% of slide height, body text cap height about 2.1% of slide height. Circle outlines about 1 px; divider rules about 1 px in #E2E4E8. Footer text grey #999999.

### Slide 8

**Text**

Contents / 01 Introduction / 02 WeWork & the Future of Work / 03 Financial & Transaction Overview / 04 Appendix / 7 / | / wework / © 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Two-column split: a full-height photograph fills the left 38.3% of the slide edge to edge, and the right column holds a left-aligned title and a numbered four-item contents list on a white field. The title sits at the top of the right column, the list sits below it with even vertical spacing, and the footer runs along the bottom right with the page number, a vertical divider, the wework logo and the copyright line, all right-aligned to a margin of about 2% of the slide width.

**Visuals**

Photograph on the left: an interior stairwell viewed from a high vantage point looking down a staircase. White vertical-baluster railings line both sides, with wooden treads and a blue handrail on the lower right. A red painted wall stripe runs down the left and a red pipe is visible at the top. A glowing neon sign of a flower in a vase hangs in the centre background, in pink, purple, blue and yellow. Green potted plants are at the upper right and in the mid background, and a round mirror is set in a concrete wall. A woman in a beige long coat walks down the stairs, motion-blurred, at the centre-lower area. Bright daylight enters from windows at the top left. Dominant colours are white, warm wood brown, red, and green. The image is full-bleed to the top, bottom and left edges, cropped to the 38.3% column, with no overlay or tint. No other graphics, icons or charts are present; eleven tiny 2 by 2 pixel placeholder images sit in the right column at the positions listed in the elements, and they are not visible.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the whole slide, with the photograph covering the left 38.3%. Clean white background with a sans-serif geometric typeface, likely Gilroy or a similar geometric grotesque. Title Contents is in black #000000, semibold, large (about 6.0% of slide height). Contents list items are in medium weight grey #B3B3B3, about 3.3% of slide height. The active item, 02 WeWork & the Future of Work, is in teal #3FA7C3. Item numbers are in light weight grey #B3B3B3 at about 2.0% of slide height. The copyright line is bold, about 1.2% of slide height, grey #999999. The wework logo is dark wordmark #1A1A1A in a bold serif. The page number 7 is dark #1A1A1A. A teal #3FA7C3 rectangle lies behind the photograph and is hidden by it. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners or outlines are used.

### Slide 10

**Text**

Our core values
Our Values
How We Live Them
Do the right thing.
Strive to be better, together.
Be entrepreneurial.
Give gratitude.
Be human, be kind.
We know the “right thing” is a deliberate action we must always take, and that
it is based on integrity and builds trust with those who we are about, including
our people, members, and our community.
We’ve always believed that we are better together. We must operate with a
shared purpose to constantly improve and grow and to become better as
individuals, as teams, and as a company.
To reshape the way the world works, we must be bold, act with courage, and
demonstrate the resiliency to push ahead no matter the odds or the
circumstance.
We will not take anyone or anything for granted. We are grateful for our people,
members, and our greater community as well as for the opportunities we have
in front of us.
Collaboration, kindness, and authenticity are essential to our humanity. We
must cherish each other and build a community that celebrates each person’s
unique talents, passions, and backgrounds.
9
|
© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

A single title sits top-left at about x 2.7%, y 6% on a white field, above two columns separated by a wide gutter. The left column (headed 'Our Values', centred over a blue rule) holds five teal bold statements aligned to the same left edge at x 5.2%; the right column (headed 'How We Live Them', centred over a longer blue rule) holds five left-aligned body paragraphs starting at x 39.7%, each paragraph vertically aligned with its matching value. Left margin about 2.7%, right margin about 4%. Footer at bottom right: page number 9, a thin vertical separator, the wework wordmark, and the copyright line beneath it right-aligned at the bottom margin.

**Visuals**

Two thin horizontal blue rules, one under 'Our Values' (x 3.5% to 32.9%, y about 24%) and one under 'How We Live Them' (x 38.2% to 95.2%, y about 24%), solid, about 2 px stroke, colour #1BA1CB. A small black serif lowercase 'wework' wordmark logo at bottom right beside the page number 9. No photographs, charts or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background, #FFFFFF, with no gradient, texture or imagery. Blue rules #1BA1CB. Footer page number #2B2B33 and copyright in bold sans-serif #999999 about 1.2% of height. No shapes with corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at full left margin (about 2.7% from the left edge), with a one-line subtitle beneath it. Below is a three-column grid: each column has a solid colour header band with a white line icon and caption, then a large numeric stat over a label, then a thin blue rule; the two stat rows are aligned across all three columns. Footnotes are left-aligned at the bottom left, and the page number, a vertical divider, the wework logo and the copyright line sit bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three solid header bands, each with a white line icon above a white caption. Left band (#3FA7C3) has a white line-art globe with meridian and latitude lines. Middle band (#007394) has a white line-art skyline of several office buildings of varying heights with window lines. Right band (#005056) has a white line-art outline of three people (one centred and taller, two flanking) with round heads and rounded shoulders. Below the bands are six large blue numerals with labels, in two rows of three: 850+ / Locations(1); 1M+ / Workstations(1)(3); 450k+ / 2020 Memberships(1)(4); 150+ / Cities(1); $3B+ / 2021E Revenue(2); 50%+ / of total memberships are Enterprise(1). Each stat column has a thin blue horizontal rule beneath its second row and beneath its first row. No charts, photographs or tints.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or imagery. Title in dark charcoal #2B2D35, regular-weight geometric sans-serif (Gotham/Gilroy-like), title cap height about 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle in the same charcoal #2B2D35, same family, regular weight, about 2.8% em. Header captions white #FFFFFF, medium weight, about 2.5% em. Stat numerals in bold-leaning blue #0A89B1, geometric sans, about 9.5% em. Stat labels charcoal #2B2D35, regular, about 2.8% em. Footnotes charcoal #2B2D35, regular, about 1.5% em. Rules are thin blue #0A89B1 lines. Header bands are flat rectangles with square corners and no shadow. Copyright line in bold grey #999999 at 1.23% em.

### Slide 12

**Text**

WeWork platform today / Leading brand-name flexible workplace provider with global infrastructure in-place to scale as occupancy rebounds / US and Canada / 367 / EMEA / 147 / China / 103 / Japan / 36 / India / 34 / LatAm / 90 / Pacific / 74 / # Total Locations as of Year End 2020 / Global Customers / amazon / salesforce / GE / NETFLIX / Microsoft / TESLA / Johnson & Johnson / snowflake / verizon / Walmart / PELOTON / Goldman Sachs / Square / BLACKROCK / Spotify / UnitedHealthcare / J.P.Morgan / IBM / MERCK / airbnb / Global Landlord Partnerships / The Blackstone Group / bxp Boston Properties / Brookfield Properties / Allianz / HBC / Columbia Property Trust / Hines / LIONSTONE INVESTMENTS / RXR / STARWOOD CAPITAL GROUP / TISHMAN SPEYER / vanke 万科 / 11 / wework / © 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a world map occupies the left of the slide (roughly x 0 to 71%, y 17 to 96%) with the title and subtitle stacked across the top-left, and two stacked logo panels in a right-hand column (x 70 to 97%) headed Global Customers and Global Landlord Partnerships, each with a thin grey rule under its heading. Title is left-aligned at x 2.7% and y 6.1%; the footnote sits bottom-centre-left under the map; the page number 11, a vertical bar, the wework wordmark and the copyright line sit bottom-right, right-aligned to the about 97% margin.

**Visuals**

A bubble map of the world on a flat light-grey (#E8E8E8) continent silhouette with no borders or labels, showing about 36 translucent circles in teal-to-navy tones (#2A8BB0 light teal through #1F4E63 dark navy, some near #1B2F3A), sized by location count. Region labels sit beside clusters: US and Canada 367 (largest cluster, several large light-teal bubbles over North America), EMEA 147 (blue bubbles over Northern and Western Europe, one over the Middle East), China 103 (large navy bubbles in eastern China), Japan 36 (dark bubble just east of Japan), India 34 (single brown-olive bubble #5A4628 on India), LatAm 90 (navy bubbles down South America), Pacific 74 (grey-violet bubbles #7A7A8A over Australia and Southeast Asia). Right column contains 20 customer logos in a 4-column grid and 12 landlord logos in a 3-column grid, all in their own brand colours (amazon black with orange arrow, salesforce blue, GE blue monogram, NETFLIX red, Microsoft four-colour flag, TESLA red, Johnson & Johnson red script, snowflake blue, verizon black with red check, Walmart blue with yellow spark, PELOTON black, Goldman Sachs black, Square black, BLACKROCK black, Spotify green, UnitedHealthcare blue, J.P.Morgan black, IBM blue striped, MERCK green and black, airbnb red; Blackstone black and white, bxp blue-grey, Brookfield navy, Allianz blue, HBC white on navy block, Columbia blue-green, Hines red, Lionstone grey lion, RXR navy, Starwood blue square, Tishman Speyer grey, vanke red with Chinese characters). No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide. Title in near-black #111111 sans-serif (Gotham-like geometric sans, regular weight), about 4.4% of slide height. Subtitle in near-black #111111 sans-serif regular, about 2.3% of slide height. Section headings Global Customers and Global Landlord Partnerships in dark grey #333333 sans-serif regular, about 2.3%. ### Slide 13

**Text**

Key investment highlights
Large Addressable Market
Multi-trillion dollar global office real estate market with COVID tailwinds for flexible workspace options
Focused Core Business Model
Strong unit economics with clear path to profitability expected by Q4 2021
Future Of Work Opportunity
Digitization of real estate and asset light platform offering expected to further drive revenue growth and margin
Compelling Value Proposition
Offering customers flexibility of space and time, portability of cost, and expense savings
Significant Competitive Advantage
Global infrastructure with $15 billion+ moat of invested capital has created a strong brand and membership base
Results-driven Leadership Team
Turnaround track record with proven public company experience
12
wework
© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Six-item grid of 3 columns by 2 rows on a white slide. The title sits top-left at about 2.7% left margin and 6% from the top, left-aligned. Each grid cell has a centred circular icon above a centred bold heading and a centred two- to three-line body paragraph. The page number, a vertical divider and the wework logo sit bottom-right, with the copyright line beneath them, right-aligned to about 97.5% of slide width.

**Visuals**

Six flat line icons, each white line-art on a solid teal circle (#3FA7C3). Top-left icon: a set of axes with an upward arrow and a dollar sign at the arrow tip. Top-centre icon: a dollar sign inside a square bracketed frame. Top-right icon: a zigzag rising trend line with an arrowhead at the upper right. Bottom-left icon: a circular loop above a horizontal line ending in an arrow pointing right. Bottom-centre icon: a globe inside a ring with a small node dot at the upper left of the ring. Bottom-right icon: three person silhouettes inside a ring with a small node dot at the upper left. No photographs, charts or tables. Each icon is about 15% of slide width in diameter, unboxed and not cropped.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 14

**Text**

Refocused and the path forward / After a significant turnaround in 2020, WeWork is now positioned for profitable growth as a leader in global flexible workplace / 2020 / Going Forward / Focus / Core WeWork(1) / Expand beyond core via All Access, Marketplace, and Platform offerings / Strategy / Disciplined focus on cost reduction, identifying the path to profitability, and exiting non-core business lines and underperforming assets / Focus on profitable market share expansion, increasing ARPM and asset light buildouts / Business Model / Offering that creates flexibility of space, time, and portability of cost as companies move away from long-term leases / Enhance asset light revenue growth through digitization of real estate and platform offerings / Leadership / Proven results-driven executives with success across real estate and subscription-based business models / Additional leadership expertise in platform building through BowX / Customer Base / Mix of small and medium-sized businesses, with a shift towards Enterprise / Primarily Enterprise, with a mix of small and medium-sized businesses / 1. Represents Core Leased as defined in the Definitions page in the appendix. / 13 | wework / © 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Two-column comparison table on a white slide with a left-aligned title at top-left (x 2.7%, y 6.1%) and a left-aligned subtitle beneath it (y 14.2%). A row-label column sits at x 2.7% to about 16%, a 2020 column at x 17.8% to 49.8%, and a Going Forward column at x 63.3% to 95.3%, with five rows stacked from y 31.8% to 88% on a regular vertical rhythm (row pitch 11.3%). Column headers '2020' and 'Going Forward' sit above thin rules at y about 30.5%. Footnote '1.' sits bottom-left at y 96.1%; the page number '13', a vertical separator and the wework wordmark sit bottom-right, with the copyright line beneath them at y 96.8%. Margins are about 2.7% left and 2.5% right.

**Visuals**

Diagram/table-style graphic with five horizontal rows of paired rectangles and arrows; no photographs or charts. Each row has a left-hand light-teal box (2020 state) and a right-hand saturated-blue box (Going Forward state), joined by a teal horizontal arrow pointing right. Row 1 (Focus, y 31.8%): left box 'Core WeWork(1)', right box 'Expand beyond core via All Access, Marketplace, and Platform offerings'. Row 2 (Strategy, y 43.2%): left 'Disciplined focus on cost reduction, identifying the path to profitability, and exiting non-core business lines and underperforming assets'; right 'Focus on profitable market share expansion, increasing ARPM and asset light buildouts'. Row 3 (Business Model, y 54.5%): left 'Offering that creates flexibility of space, time, and portability of cost as companies move away from long-term leases'; right 'Enhance asset light revenue growth through digitization of real estate and platform offerings'. Row 4 (Leadership, y 65.8%): left 'Proven results-driven executives with success across real estate and subscription-based business models'; right 'Additional leadership expertise in platform building through BowX'. Row 5 (Customer Base, y 77.1%): left 'Mix of small and medium-sized businesses, with a shift towards Enterprise'; right 'Primarily Enterprise, with a mix of small and medium-sized businesses'. Arrows are straight lines about 6.4% wide with filled triangular heads, at x 53.4% to 59.8%, centred on each row. Two thin teal rules under the column headers. The wework wordmark is a black bold serif logotype at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide. Background white #FFFFFF. Title in near-black #000000, subtitle in near-black #000000. Column headers and row labels in teal #43B3CA. Left row boxes filled #E1F1F3 with dark teal body text #1A5A66. Right row boxes filled #0087B1 with white #FFFFFF body text. Arrows and header rules in teal #43B3CA, about 0.3% of slide height thick, no arrowhead outline. Typeface: geometric humanist sans-serif similar to Gotham, title in medium weight, row labels in medium weight, body text regular. Title about 5.0% of slide height, subtitle about 2.3%, row labels about 3.6%, headers about 3.0%, body text about 2.1%, footnote and copyright about 1.2%. Boxes are sharp-cornered rectangles with no shadows or gradients and no borders. Text in boxes is left-aligned with roughly 3% inner left padding.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Review: Most main-body slides pair a headline claim with a supporting chart, as on slides 15, 24, 28 and 33, and the template, palette and footer are consistent.
- Worth keeping: Keep the claim-style headlines with a supporting chart (slides 15, 24, 28), the consistent teal palette and footer, the section divider pages, and the large-number KPI tiles on slides 11, 16 and 18.

**Text**

2021: Repositioned and on the path to recovery / Recent cost optimization efforts have set the Company up for profitable growth in 2021 and beyond / Total Workstations / (in thousands) / Ending Physical Occupancy(1) : / 72% / 46% / 70% / 855 / 1,030 / 954 / 2019A / 2020A / 2021E / Strategic asset exits in 2020 and 2021 improved strength and composition of portfolio / Total Revenue / ($ in billions) / $3.2 / $3.2 / $3.2 / 2019A / 2020A / 2021E / Stable revenues despite headwinds in 2020 / Run-Rate Functional Expense Savings(2) / ($ in billions) / $2.1 / $1.0 / $0.8-$0.85 / Q4 2019A / Q4 2020A / Q4 2021E / Significant reduction in functional expenses (SG&A) in 2020 / Additional $150-$200mm of savings expected over 2021 / Source: Management Projections. / Note: KPIs include IndiaCo and ChinaCo. Financials include IndiaCo management fee and excludes ChinaCo. Please see the Definitions page in the appendix for further detail. / 1. Excludes WeMemberships. / 2. Represents the difference in annualized functional expenses from Q4'19 vs. Q4'20. Expenses reflect adjusted functional expense and includes general and administrative, sales and marketing, sourcing and development, and other. Excludes stock based compensation and ventures expenses. / 14 / wework / © 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Three equal chart columns sit side by side under a full-width title block, with the left column at about 2.7% to 30% of slide width, the middle at about 36% to 64% and the right at about 67% to 97%. The title is left-aligned at the top margin, with a subtitle beneath it; each column has a bold heading, a unit line, a bar chart with a baseline and a takeaway bullet below, and a footnote block with source, note and numbered notes sits at the bottom left with the page number and wework logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three vertical bar charts. Chart 1: type bar chart, title Total Workstations, unit (in thousands), categories 2019A, 2020A, 2021E, labelled values 855, 1,030 and 954 (bars, no axis scale printed), above a separate row of pale aqua ovals labelled 72%, 46% and 70% titled Ending Physical Occupancy(1). Chart 2: vertical bar chart, title Total Revenue, unit ($ in billions), categories 2019A, 2020A, 2021E, labelled values $3.2, $3.2 and $3.2 (equal bar heights, no axis scale printed). Chart 3: vertical bar chart, title Run-Rate Functional Expense Savings(2), unit ($ in billions), categories Q4 2019A, Q4 2020A and Q4 2021E, labelled values $2.1, $1.0 and $0.8-$0.85; the Q4 2021E bar carries a dashed teal outline drawn as a range placeholder above its solid bar, with the bar for Q4 2021E at the lowest height. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the dashed outline. Bars are flat solid fills with no gridlines, no value axis and a single dark baseline under each chart.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no texture. Titles and headings are near-black #111111. Body and bullet text is dark grey #222222. Chart bars are deep cerulean #0087B1 in the first two charts and lighter teal #3FA7C3 in the third chart. Occupancy ovals are pale aqua #E5F6F9 with no outline. Footnote text is mid grey #555555. The wework wordmark is black #000000. Typeface is a geometric humanist sans-serif similar to Gotham or Avenir, regular weight for values and body, bold for chart headings and the 2021 title in regular-to-medium weight. Title text is about 5.4% of slide height, subtitle about 2.8%, bullets about 3.2%, chart headings about 2.8%, value labels about 2.6%, footnotes about 1.2%. Bars have square corners, no shadows, no gradients. Baselines are thin dark grey lines about 0.2% of height thick.

### Slide 16

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the claim-style headlines with a supporting chart (slides 15, 24, 28), the consistent teal palette and footer, the section divider pages, and the large-number KPI tiles on slides 11, 16 and 18.

**Text**

2020: Optimized cost structure and core business
~$1.1B
Functional Expense Savings(1)
~$400M
OpEx Savings(2)
$200M+
Savings from 200+ Lease / Exits and Amendments(3)
conductor / FLATIRON SCHOOL / Meetup / Q / Space IQ / SPACIOUS / team / waltz
Exited Non-Core Businesses
Source: Management Projections.
1. Represents the difference in annualized functional expenses from Q4 2019 to Q4 2020. Expenses reflect adjusted functional expense and includes general and administrative, sales and marketing, sourcing and development, and other. Excludes stock based compensation and ventures expenses.
2. Calculated based on full year location operating expenses (including rent, tenancy and building expenses) per USF reduction from Q4'19 to Q4'20 multiplied by Q4 2020 USF. Savings of ~$400M mainly due to reduced foot traffic, restructuring of WeWork's community team and ongoing rent renegotiations in 2020. Location operating expense savings calculation excludes stock based compensation, community support and venture expenses.
3. Lease restructurings and renegotiations as of January 2021. Savings include rent and tenancy.
15 | wework
© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Single content slide with a left-aligned title at the top-left (x about 2.7%, y about 6%) on a white field. Below it sits a 2x2 grid of pale blue boxes centred to the right of the slide: top-left box at x 14.2% and top-right box at x 50.0%, both starting at y 17.0% with width 34.1% and height about 32%; bottom row starts at y about 51% with the same widths, leaving a gap of about 1.7% between columns and rows. Each box's content is centred horizontally. Source notes and numbered footnotes run full-width at the bottom-left from x 2.7% (y about 87% to 97%), left-aligned. Page number '15', a divider bar, and the wework wordmark sit bottom-right, with the copyright line beneath them.

**Visuals**

Four pale blue rectangular panels with square corners and no shadows. Top-left panel: large teal numeral '~$1.1B' with the label 'Functional Expense Savings' and superscript (1). Top-right panel: large teal '~$400M' with label 'OpEx Savings' and superscript (2). Bottom-left panel: large teal '$200M+' with two-line label 'Savings from 200+ Lease / Exits and Amendments' and superscript (3). Bottom-right panel: a grid of eight company logos, rows as follows. Row 1: conductor (green dotted icon plus black lowercase wordmark), FLATIRON SCHOOL (blue slanted strokes plus dark grey uppercase stacked wordmark), Meetup (red-pink handwritten wordmark). Row 2: a dark grey Q glyph, Space IQ (orange-brown cube icon plus dark grey wordmark), SPACIOUS (black wide sans uppercase). Row 3: team (black lowercase wordmark), waltz (blue-violet wordmark with a small dotted icon). Below the logos is the caption 'Exited Non-Core Businesses'. No photographs, charts or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Panels are pale blue #D9EDF3. Big numerals and the title use strong colours: numerals are teal #0A8AAB; the title and footnote text are near-black #111111; panel labels are dark slate #2D3640. Typeface is a geometric humanist sans-serif similar to Gilroy or Avenir, regular weight for labels and medium for numerals; title is roughly 4.8% of slide height, numerals about 9% of slide height, labels about 3.4%, footnotes about 1.3%. Line and shape styles: flat fills, no borders, no gradients, no shadows, square corners. Copyright text is light grey #999999 bold.

**Layout**

Three content columns sit under a left-aligned title at the top left (x 2.7%, y 6.5%), with the SMB and MLB columns grouped by a teal brace and a large plus sign between the MLB and Enterprise columns. Column headings are centered above thin grey rules, and the photographs share one top edge at about 28.6% of slide height. A centered teal tagline runs across the lower third, a source note sits bottom left at x 3.5%, and the page number, wordmark and copyright sit bottom right at about 88% to 96% width.

**Visuals**

Three rectangular photographs, all sharp-edged, unrounded and unbordered, each the same height and aligned on a shared top edge. (1) Left photo, SMB column: a man in a light blue shirt and dark jeans sits on the left of a grey-blue modular sofa with a laptop open on a round dark table; behind him are tall fiddle-leaf plants, a blue wall and a wood-panelled staircase; a cream cushion with black stripes sits on the sofa; two woven cane chairs are in the foreground. Full-colour, daylight from the upper left, mostly green, blue, wood and grey tones. (2) Centre photo, MLB column: five people in a bright meeting room, a man in a grey shirt seated at a long white table with a red base, a man in a light blue shirt and a woman in black stand at the right, a woman in a white top stands at a whiteboard covered in handwriting, with laptops and papers on the table, orange-cushioned chairs in the foreground, and an orange pendant lamp hanging from the top; window light from the right; white, grey, red and orange tones. (3) Right photo, Enterprise column: a large open-plan office with a long dark wooden table and brown upholstered chairs in the foreground, a man in a grey suit talking on a phone at the left, a man in a pink shirt and a woman in orange standing at the back, floor-to-ceiling windows and a city skyline behind, pendant lamps overhead, and a 'JUICE' sign with flowers at the far right; neutral wood, grey and orange tones with daylight from the windows. Decorative elements: a thin grey horizontal rule above each column heading; a teal curly brace (curly bracket shape, opening upward) below the SMB and MLB photos, its tips rising to the photo bottom edges and its centre point projecting down toward the figures below; a bold teal plus sign between the MLB and Enterprise photos; a small black wordmark 'wework' in the bottom right corner.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF covering the full slide.

1. Total sales pipeline represents potential total contract value of sales opportunities created by our salesforce as of February 2021. **Layout**

Single-slide layout on a white field with the title and subtitle left-aligned at the top margin (about 2.7% from the left edge). A flywheel of four curved arrows sits in the right half, centred around x 61%, y 50%, with two large stacked figures and their captions on the left half. The footnote block runs across the bottom left, with the page number 17, a small wework logo and the copyright line in the bottom right corner.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a circular flywheel made of four thick curved arrows in #3FA7C3, arranged clockwise. Each arc has an arrowhead at its clockwise end: the top arc ends at top right pointing right, the right arc ends at lower right pointing down, the bottom arc ends at bottom left pointing left, and the left arc ends at upper left pointing up. Inside the circle, from top to bottom: the text 'Global customer growth increases occupancy rates for landlords...' in blue, the wework wordmark in dark charcoal, and the text '...driving demand from landlords, increasing utility for our customers, and further enhancing customer growth' in blue. Left side: two large numerals in blue, '~$4.0B+' above the label 'Total sales pipeline(1)' and '~$1.5B' above the label '2021E committed revenue (2)'. Logo: the wework wordmark in dark charcoal at the centre of the flywheel and at the bottom right. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background is plain white #FFFFFF with no gradient or texture. Title is near-black #111111, semi-bold geometric sans-serif (Gotham-style), sized about 5% of slide height. Subtitle is #111111 regular weight, about 2.4% of slide height. Large numerals are blue #1A8BB5, light-weight geometric sans, about 11% of slide height. Labels under numerals are charcoal #2B2B2B, regular weight, about 3.5% of slide height. Flywheel arcs are #3FA7C3 with round caps and filled arrowheads. Circle text is blue #1A8BB5, regular weight, about 2.9% of slide height. Footnotes are charcoal #333333 about 1.3% of slide height. Copyright line is #999999 bold about 1.2% of slide height. No shadows, corner radii or gradients.

### Slide 19

**Text**

Membership composition highlights the quality improvement of the business
Focused on developing long-term, high-value Enterprise customers, laying the foundation for predictable and prudent growth
2015A
2020A
Membership
Physical Memberships(1)
35k+
450k+
Enterprise Mix(2) (% of Physical Memberships)
10%+
50%+
Commitment Length(3)
Month-to-Month
100%
~10%

0%
50%+
Total Weighted Full Commitment Length
~1 month
15+ months
Note: All membership and occupancy metrics include IndiaCo and ChinaCo. Reflects ending figures as of 12/31 of each respective year.
1. Physical Membership defined as the number of people able to access WeWork's locations. Does not include All Access memberships.
2. Enterprise Memberships are defined as organizations who have +500 full time employees globally. Enterprise membership percentage represents total enterprise memberships excluding WeMemberships divided by total physical memberships, which excludes WeMemberships.
3. Commitment length represents base contract terms, excluding the impact of any extension and / or termination options. The commitment lengths disclosed may include periods for which members have an option to terminate their commitments with a less than 10% penalty.
18 | wework
© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

A single full-width title sits top-left at the top margin, with a one-line subtitle beneath it, both left-aligned to the same left margin of about 2.7% of slide width. The main content is a three-column comparison table: row labels left-aligned at about 4.5% to 5.9%, the 2015A column centred near 54% and the 2020A column centred near 84%, with a blue arrow between them in each row, and two section groups (Membership, Commitment Length). Footnotes sit bottom-left in a small block from about 87% to 97% of slide height; the page number '18', a vertical bar, the wework wordmark and the copyright line sit bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Not a chart or photograph; a comparison table with three columns and two row groups. Columns: row label (left), 2015A (centre column), a blue right-pointing arrow in each row (centre-right, between columns), 2020A (right). Column headers: 2015A and 2020A, each with a thin light-grey underline beneath. Group 1 heading: Membership. Rows: Physical Memberships(1) with 2015A value 35k+ and 2020A value 450k+; Enterprise Mix(2) (% of Physical Memberships) with 10%+ and 50%+. Group 2 heading: Commitment Length(3). Rows: Month-to-Month with 100% and ~10%; 12 Month+ with 0% and 50%+; Total Weighted Full Commitment Length with ~1 month and 15+ months. Each of the five data rows has an arrow shape of identical style: a thin teal-blue line with a solid triangular arrowhead, running horizontally from about 65% to 72% of slide width. No highlighted points, no legend, no photographs, no logos other than the wework wordmark in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background, flat with no gradients, shadows or textures. Title and row labels and values in dark charcoal #2B2D3A; section headings (Membership, Commitment Length) and column headers (2015A, 2020A) and arrows in teal-blue #1B9AC6. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif similar to Gotham or Gilroy, regular weight for body and values, medium weight for the title; title is large (about 5% of slide height cap-to-descender), subtitle and row labels about 2.6% to 3.6%, values about 3.6% to 4.2%, footnotes about 1.2% to 1.4% of slide height. Thin light-grey #C8C8C8 rules under the column headers, no other shapes or rounded corners. Footnote and superscript text in dark grey #3A3A3A; copyright line in light grey #999999 bold.

© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Single-column header with the title left-aligned at about 2.7% from the left edge, a subtitle line beneath it, then a teal section heading with a thin full-width rule. Below the rule is a two-column grid of icon-and-text items: the left column starts at about 8% (icons) and 15% (text), the right column at about 51% (icons) and 59% (text). The left column holds three items, the right column two items plus a row of two logos. The page number, a vertical separator and the wework wordmark sit bottom right at about 88% to 99% width and 94% to 98% height, with the copyright line beneath them, right-aligned.

**Visuals**

Five line icons in a single dark grey stroke (#333333), roughly 1.5 px line weight, no fills. (1) Drafting compass/divider icon at upper left of the left column, a circular pivot at the top above two splayed legs with a crossbar. (2) Circular dollar-sign icon at upper right, a dollar sign inside a circle formed by two arcs with small arrowheads at the top-left and bottom-right, implying cycling exchange. (3) Upward zigzag trend line with an arrowhead at the top right, representing growth, in the middle left. (4) Face mask icon in the middle right, a mask outline with pleat lines and ear loops. (5) Globe icon with latitude and longitude lines inside a ring, with a small circle and tail at the upper left, lower left of the left column. Two logos sit under the right column text: (a) a circular emblem in grey with a figure and the Latin motto text around the ring, the year 1929 at the bottom, and a grey label bar reading PLANT SAFETY below it, in black/grey tones, about 5.6% wide by 9.8% high. (b) INTERNATIONAL SOS wordmark in bold black sans-serif with a swoosh arc passing behind the letters and the tagline WORLDWIDE REACH. HUMAN TOUCH. in tiny capitals underneath, about 5.6% wide by 5.7% high. No photographs. No charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF) with no gradients, shadows or textures. Title in near-black (#000000) geometric sans-serif (close to Gotham, medium weight), about 5% of slide height for cap-height-based size. Subtitle in near-black (#000000) regular weight, about 2% of slide height. Section heading in teal (#1B8FB0) medium weight, about 3.3% of slide height. Body text dark grey (#333333) regular weight about 2.4% of slide height, with key phrases in lighter teal (#2AA5C4). Thin divider rule in light grey (#CCCCCC), 0.1% high. Icons in dark grey (#333333) stroke, square corners on no shapes. Footer page number in black regular at about 1.2% height; copyright in grey (#999999), bold, about 1.2% height. No corner radii or shadows.

Notes: "Standard lease" cost illustration includes "buildout" costs associated with construction, procurement, and design services, and "operations" costs including base rent, utilities, insurance, property taxes, and facilities management among others shown on page. WeWork cost per employee reflects proposed WeWork membership fee per employee per year, illustrating the WeWork value proposition to select prospective enterprise members relative to current lease costs and / or market norms. Both illustrative examples are representative of the "WeWork pitch" to select prospective enterprise members in the markets or situations outlined. The percentage savings shown are examples of specific situations and / or market studies, and may not be representative of every individual member situation. Results of the Amsterdam and Seattle case studies cited above may not be replicable with other clients or in other markets. 2020E is based on Q4 2020E preliminary results and is subject to change.
1. Comparison data represents annualized costs based on a 96-month scenario for Seattle market standard lease specifically formulated for a potential enterprise member company. Based on publicly available lease cost information and third-party market research at time of interaction for proposed WeWork alternative. Buildout / standard lease cost calculated as of Q3 2020.
2. Comparison data represents annualized costs based on a 60-month scenario for Amsterdam specifically formulated for a potential member company. Based on existing / prior lease cost of potential member at time of interaction for proposed WeWork alternative. Buildout / standard lease cost calculated as of Q4 2020.
20
wework
© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Single slide with a left-aligned title and subtitle across the top, then two side-by-side bar-chart panels (Seattle on the left, Amsterdam on the right) with a row of category labels at the far left. Each panel has a bold-free header with a thin grey rule beneath it. Footnotes sit full-width at the bottom left, with the page number, a separator, the wework wordmark and the confidentiality line in the bottom-right corner. Margins are about 2.7% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Two paired vertical stacked bar charts, one per case study. Seattle: a standard lease bar stacked from bottom to top as 13,000 (dark teal #007394) and 2,090 (light teal #3FA7C3), total $15,090(1); the bar is labelled 'Standard lease' below. Beside it, a grey #5B5B6B 'All included' bar at $11,475(1), labelled with the wework wordmark below. A teal connector line runs from the $15,090 label to a cream oval reading '24% savings', and a small teal down-arrow points from the oval to $11,475. Amsterdam: a standard lease bar stacked as 6,534 (dark teal #007394) and 4,100 (light teal #3FA7C3), total € 10,634(2), labelled 'Standard lease' below. Beside it, a grey 'All included' bar at € 7,726(2), labelled with the wework wordmark. A teal connector line runs to a cream oval reading '27% savings' with a down-arrow to € 7,726. Bars are flat with no gradients or shadows. Bar heights are proportional to values, with the grey bars slightly shorter. Left side: a teal curly brace spanning Design, Construction and FF&E procurement (Buildout, teal heading) and a second teal brace spanning Base rent through Events (Operations, teal heading). No legend and no axes or gridlines; the baseline under each bar pair is a thin light grey line.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title in dark charcoal #2A2B33, regular weight, geometric sans-serif (Gotham-like), about 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle and body text in dark charcoal #2A2B33, regular weight, about 2.3% of slide height. Panel headers in dark charcoal #2A2B33, about 2.2%. Accents in teal #007394, light teal #3FA7C3, and percentage figures in teal #0F9BC4. Category labels are italic light weight, dark grey #2A2B33, about 1.6%. Footnotes are about 1.1%, grey #5B5B6B. Cream oval fills #F6F2EC with no outline. Thin grey rules #C8C8CC, 1 px. Wordmark is a heavy, black, custom logotype in #2A2B33. Corner radii: none on bars, fully rounded on ovals. No shadows or gradients.

**Text**

WeWork’s platform opportunity
WeWork’s scale and innovation will continue to lead the flexible workspace market
Space-as-a-Service
Offering that creates flexibility of space,
time, and portability of cost, as companies
move away from long-term leases
Proven membership base and occupancy
projected to normalize to historical levels
Right-sized operations have created
operating leverage, a path to profitability
and compelling unit economics
COVID has accelerated the shift to flexible
workspace
Digitization of Real Estate
All Access offering digitizes WeWork spaces
and provides end customers with ultimate
flexibility
Single membership card allows employees
to “Work From Anywhere”
Marketplace offering strengthens
membership through high-margin, value-
add services
Platform
Third party flexible workspace
management services leveraging
WeWork’s property and technology
platform
Flexible and capital light growth
alternatives
High operating leverage catalyzes
attractive cash flow growth and margin
expansion
21
|
wework
© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Three stepped columns of roughly equal width (about 26% of slide width each) start at the left margin (about 2.7%) and rise from left to right, each with an elbow arrow above a section heading, a hairline rule and bullet text. The title sits top-left at about 7% from the top with the subtitle beneath it, both left-aligned on the 2.7% margin. The footer sits bottom-right with the page number, a vertical divider, the wework wordmark and the copyright line; there is no image.

**Visuals**

Three filled elbow arrows, each a thick block arrow that rises from a vertical stem at its lower left, turns through a rounded inner corner and runs right to an arrowhead. Arrow 1 (Space-as-a-Service) is light blue #92D5E0 and sits lowest, arrow 2 (Digitization of Real Estate) is mid blue #0087B1 and sits higher, arrow 3 (Platform) is deep teal #006178 and sits highest. Each arrow's stem is about 2.3% of slide width and its horizontal bar about 4.1% of slide height. Under each heading is a thin grey hairline rule. No photographs, charts, tables or icons. The wework wordmark is the only logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title is near-black #000000 in a geometric humanist sans (Karla-like), regular to medium weight, about 5.4% of slide height. Subtitle is dark grey #333333, regular weight, about 2.6% of slide height. Section headings are charcoal #2B2B2B, regular weight, about 3.1% of slide height. Body text is near-black #222222, regular weight, about 1.9% of slide height, with key phrases set in blue #0087B1. Arrows use #92D5E0, #0087B1 and #006178. Hairline rules are #BBBBBB at about 1 px. No shadows, no gradients; the only corner treatment is the rounded inner corner of each arrow, roughly 4% of slide height in radius. Bullets are small black round dots.

### Slide 23

**Text**

Compelling flexibility across space, time, and cost / Space / Flexibility / 1 Desk / Custom office / Option to occupy a select number of desks or entire floors / Ability to access any workspace via All Access / Time / Flexibility / 1 Hour / Multiple years / Choice of occupying workspace on an on-demand basis, through a monthly subscription, or long-term contract / Portability / of Cost / 1 Contract / One city to another / Ability to move part or all existing commitment to a new market, region, or country / 22 / wework / © 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Single white slide with a left-aligned title at the top and three equal horizontal rows separated by thin grey rules, spanning about x 4% to 95%. Each row has three columns: a teal row label at the left (x about 7%), a centre pair of captioned icons joined by a teal arrow (x about 30% to 58%), and a right column of bullet points starting at x about 68%. The page number '22' and the wework logo sit bottom right with the copyright line beneath them; there is no visible top or left margin text beyond the title.

**Visuals**

Three rows of simple line icons with teal arrows, no chart or photograph. Row 1 (Space Flexibility): a desk-with-monitor icon captioned '1 Desk' pointing via a horizontal teal arrow to a custom office icon (a multi-tower building with a dome and a round window) captioned 'Custom office'. Row 2 (Time Flexibility): a stopwatch/clock icon captioned '1 Hour' pointing via a teal arrow to a calendar icon with two binder rings and a left-right double arrow inside, captioned 'Multiple years'. Row 3 (Portability of Cost): a document/contract scroll icon captioned '1 Contract' pointing via a teal arrow to a globe inside a circular arrow icon captioned 'One city to another'. Icons are outlined in teal #1A8FAE with about 2px strokes, no fills. Arrows are teal lines about 1px thick with solid triangular heads at the right end. Thin grey #B0B0B0 horizontal rules separate rows.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF background.

### Slide 24

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A market-sizing chart pairs a penetration curve with a clearly labelled base and high case, so the large opportunity claim is shown visually rather than stated.
- Review: Most main-body slides pair a headline claim with a supporting chart, as on slides 15, 24, 28 and 33, and the template, palette and footer are consistent.
- Worth keeping: Keep the claim-style headlines with a supporting chart (slides 15, 24, 28), the consistent teal palette and footer, the section divider pages, and the large-number KPI tiles on slides 11, 16 and 18.

**Text**

Massive growth opportunity as flexible workspace market expands
WeWork has an opportunity to grow flexible workspace market share in the multi-trillion dollar global office real estate market
U.S. Flexible Workspace / Penetration of 3.5 billion Sq. Ft. / Office Space(1)
High Case TAM: / 22.2%
Base Case TAM: / 13.3%
Implied wework :
High: 5.6%
Base: 3.3%
2010 2012 2014 2016 2018 2020 2022 2024 2026 2028 2030
25%
share of total flexible / workspace market(1)
~5x
Increase in / revenue by 2030(2)
Source: CBRE Research, Q2 2019 (Pre-COVID), Green Street Advisors, Management Projections.
Note: High case and implied WeWork high case penetration shown for illustrative purposes only. 2020E is based on Q4 2020E preliminary results and is subject to change.
1. Illustrative TAM analysis based on pre-COVID flexible workspace projections for U.S. only. Potential for significant upside in EMEA and Japan markets.; Implied total flexible workspace square footage based on estimated 2.5% 2020E flexible workspace penetration as percentage of total office space; Total office estimate based on Green Street estimate of 2019 total office square footage (3.5bn); WeWork USC usable square footage as of 2020E was 22.0mm, which implies 25.2% share of total flexible workspace market.
2. Assumes total office market square footage of 3.5bn and WeWork flexible workspace market share remains constant in the projection period. Based on projected base case flexible workspace penetration.
23
wework
© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Single-column headline band with the title at top left and a subtitle beneath it, left-aligned to a margin of about 2.7% of slide width. A chart occupies the left and centre (about 2.7% to 58% of width) with its heading at top left of the chart area, and two stacked callout boxes sit in a right column from 73% to 97% width. Source and footnotes run full width along the bottom, with the page number and WeWork logo bottom right and the copyright line beneath them.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled U.S. Flexible Workspace Penetration of 3.5 billion Sq. Ft. Office Space(1). Horizontal axis: years 2010 to 2030 at 2-year intervals (Arial, black); no axis title or unit shown; no vertical axis labels or range printed. One solid series (light-medium blue, about #2BA6C9) runs from 2010 to about 2019 at low values (about 1% rising to about 4% by 2018, read visually). Three dashed projection series rise from about 2019 to 2030: High Case TAM (darker blue about #0A7BB0) ends at 22.2%; Base Case TAM (pale cyan about #8FD6E3) ends at 13.3%; Implied WeWork High (dark olive-brown about #8C7233) ends at 5.6%; Implied WeWork Base (tan about #C9B28A) ends at 3.3%. The two WeWork lines start about 2015 (about 0.3% rising to 3.3% and 5.6%, values about; only endpoints printed). Endpoint labels sit at the right ends of the lines: High Case TAM 22.2%, Base Case TAM 13.3%, Implied wework High 5.6%, Implied wework Base 3.3%. No legend; series identified by end labels. No highlighted points. Black horizontal baseline at the bottom of the plot. Two stat boxes: a box reading 25% with caption share of total flexible workspace market(1), and a box reading ~5x with caption Increase in revenue by 2030(2).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white slide with flat light-blue stat panels, no gradients or shadows. Title in black (#000000) humanist sans-serif (Gill Sans-like), regular weight, approximately 5.5% of slide height. Subtitle in black for the first clause and dark grey (#444444) for the second clause, regular weight, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart heading in teal-blue #0A8AB8, regular weight, about 3.4% of slide height. Stat numbers 25% and ~5x in teal-blue #0A8AB8, bold, about 8% of slide height; captions in the same teal, medium weight, about 2.6%. Footnotes in dark grey #333333, about 1.3% of slide height. Chart year labels in black Arial about 1.7% of slide height. Copyright line in #999999 bold about 1.2%. Stat panels #D9EDF3 with no border or corner radius.

Significant incremental margins given subscription-based model on top of traditional leased business

**Layout**

Two-column slide: a white main area on the left (about 66% of width) and a light-blue right panel (about 34% of width, from x 65.8% to the right edge) holding four stacked feature blocks. The title All Access sits top-left at about 7% from the top with a 2.7% left margin; a two-line subtitle sits beneath it. The chart occupies the centre-left with a row of distributor logos below it, and source footnotes sit bottom-left. Page number 24, a wewoork wordmark and the copyright line sit bottom-right inside the blue panel, left-aligned to the feature text column at x 70%.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled Ending Occupancy(1) with two series across six categories on a horizontal x-axis: 2019A, 2020A, 2021E, 2022E, 2023E, 2024E. No y-axis, gridlines or numeric axis scale are printed. Series 1 Total Occupancy (dark charcoal #3A3A44 line, black labels): 72% (2019A), 47%(3) (2020A), 75% (2021E), 90% (2022E), 93% (2023E), 95% (2024E). Series 2 Physical Occupancy (blue #1B93C4 line, blue labels): about 72% at 2019A (no value printed, line starts at the same point), about 47% at 2020A (no value printed), 70% (2021E), 81% (2022E), 84% (2023E), 85% (2024E). Legend at lower right: blue line Physical Occupancy above dark line Total Occupancy. A dashed grey horizontal reference line at 85% is labelled Pre-COVID Total Occupancy: 85%(2). A bracket to the right of the 2024E endpoints spans the gap between the two lines and is labelled $481mm All Access Revenue. A thin dark horizontal baseline runs under the categories. Four line icons in the right panel: globe in a circle (blue), handshake (blue), upward growth arrows over a bar (blue), and a dollar banknote (blue). Logos: American Express, a solid blue #0072CE square with white stacked text AMERICAN EXPRESS; Uber, black lowercase wordmark on white; Brex, black icon of a bracket shape plus black Brex wordmark; Vensure Employer Services, grey-black swoosh with an orange dot above and the word Vensure in dark grey with Employer Services in orange below. The photos are none.

**Design**

Background: Two-part flat background: white #FFFFFF across the left 65.8% and pale blue #D9EDF3 across the right 34.2%. Clean corporate look on a white background with a pale-blue right panel. Title text near-black #000000 in a geometric sans (Futura-like, regular weight). Body and subtitle text near-black #1A1A1A in a light geometric sans. Section headings Ending Occupancy and Distributors and Affinity Partnerships in blue #1A8FBF, medium weight. Feature headings bold #2B2B33 in a rounded humanist sans (Karla-like). Feature body text regular #2B2B33. Accents in blue #1B93C4 for icons and the physical-occupancy series. Title approximately 5.2% of slide height; subtitle about 2.3%; feature headings about 3.0%; feature body about 2.2%; footnotes about 1.3%. Lines are thin: chart series about 3 px stroke, reference line dashed grey #8A8A93, baseline 1 px dark grey. No shadows, gradients or rounded shape corners apart from the Amex logo square.

**Layout**

Single full-width content slide on a white field with a left margin of about 2.7% and a right margin of about 2.7%. The title Marketplace sits top-left at about 6% from the top, with a one-line subtitle directly beneath it. A row of four equal-width columns sits below a horizontal header band: the first three columns are filled and sit under a Launched band spanning them, and the fourth column is outlined and sits under a separate Expected Q1 2021E outline box, with a gap of about 2.4% between columns. A Target Customer label with dotted rules above and below sits underneath the columns, with one customer line aligned under each column. The page number 25 and the wework logotype sit bottom-right, with the copyright line directly beneath them, right-aligned.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Flat white background with no gradients, shadows or textures. Titles and subtitle are near-black #111111 in a humanist sans-serif (Gill Sans style, medium weight) with the slide title at roughly 5.2% of slide height and body text at roughly 2.8% to 3.3%. Filled boxes and Launched band are teal #3FA7C3 with white text. Outline boxes, the Expected Q1 2021E label, the Insurance text and the Target Customer label use teal #0E8AAB for stroke and text. Customer lines are dark grey #333333. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadow. Rules are dotted teal #0E8AAB lines about 0.1% of height thick.

### Slide 27

**Text**

Platform / Asset light platform for managing & orchestrating flexible space while providing access to an expanded network for members / Worldwide Property Technology Platform / ● Flexible space management platform connecting enterprises and landlords / ● Single platform that provides design, build, lease, operate, manage, and community services / ● Open API ecosystem that integrates workspace, management systems, tenants, and landlords / Capital Light Model / ● Management and franchise agreements provide alternatives to existing leasing model / ● Limited upfront capital required to enter new locations and scale efficiently / ● Generates sustainable cash flow and margin expansion / Brookfield Properties / Hines / The Blackstone Group / TISHMAN SPEYER / Columbia Property Trust / bxp Boston Properties / STARWOOD CAPITAL GROUP / vanke 万科 / HBC / wework / salesforce / Goldman Sachs / verizon / citi / Square / IBM / J.P.Morgan / Walmart / TESLA / airbnb / amazon / PELOTON / Johnson & Johnson / Standard Chartered / Spotify / GE / BlackRock / MERCK / 26 / | / wework / © 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a left text column about 3% to 34% of slide width holding two headed bullet groups, and a right illustration panel about 39% to 97% wide holding a city skyline with logo cloud. The title Platform sits top-left at about 6% from the top, with a one-line subtitle beneath it spanning the full width at about 14% from the top. Left margin is about 3%. The footer page number 26, a separator bar, the wework wordmark and the copyright line sit bottom-right, with a right margin of about 2%.

**Visuals**

A black-and-white line-art illustration of a dense Manhattan-style skyline occupying about x 40% to 95%, y 28% to 78%. It shows a tall Art Deco tower with a stepped spire at left, a tall spired Empire-State-like tower at centre, a narrower Chrysler-like spired tower to its right, and many smaller rectangular and setback towers with window grids. Lines are thin dark grey (#2B2B33) on white with no fill. The skyline is cropped at its base by the arrow banner. Overlaid on the skyline are client logos in grey, dark blue, red and black. A light blue double-headed block arrow (fill #D9EDF3, no outline, very soft shadow) spans the lower skyline from about x 39.5% to 96.5%, y 66.6% to 81.3%, and carries the wework wordmark centred in dark charcoal #2B2B33. Below the arrow is a logo grid of two rows of full-colour corporate logos: row one salesforce, Goldman Sachs, verizon, citi, Square, IBM, J.P.Morgan, Walmart, Tesla; row two airbnb, amazon, PELOTON, Johnson & Johnson, Standard Chartered, Spotify, GE, BlackRock, MERCK. No photographs. Dominant colours are white, charcoal, light blue and brand colours (Salesforce blue, Walmart blue and yellow, Spotify green, airbnb coral, Tesla red, Starwood blue, HBC navy).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradients or shadows except a soft shadow under the arrow banner. Title Platform in a regular-weight geometric sans-serif similar to Gotham or Futura, dark charcoal #1B1B1F, about 5% of slide height. Subtitle in a regular-weight sans-serif, charcoal #1B1B1F, about 2.2% of slide height. Section headings in a semibold teal-blue sans-serif, #0C87B5, about 2.6% of slide height, each followed by a thin light grey #C8CDD2 rule. Body bullets in a light-weight sans-serif, dark grey #2B2B33, about 2.3% of slide height, with teal #1A8AB8 for highlighted phrases. Bullets are solid round dots. Footer page number in regular charcoal and the copyright line in light grey #999999 at about 1.2% of slide height. Arrow banner fill #D9EDF3 with rounded-free sharp points.

**Layout**

Single-column slide with the title left-aligned at the top-left margin (about 2.7% from left, top band at 6% to 13%), a subtitle line directly beneath it, and a thin grey horizontal rule separating the header from a full-width chart area that spans about 2.7% to 97% of the width. Eleven category columns sit on a shared horizontal zero baseline; a dashed vertical divider at about 79% of the width separates the 2022E Q4 column from the two forecast years 2023E and 2024E. The footnotes are left-aligned at the bottom-left margin, and the page number, wework wordmark and copyright line sit in the bottom-right corner.

**Visuals**

Chart: combined column and dot chart, untitled apart from the subtitle 'Ending Occupancy(1) and Adj. EBITDA Margin Over Time'. No y-axis scale or gridlines are printed; all values are data labels. X-axis categories left to right: 2020A Q4, 2021E Q1, 2021E Q2, 2021E Q3, 2021E Q4, 2022E Q1, 2022E Q2, 2022E Q3, 2022E Q4, 2023E, 2024E. Series 1, Adj. EBITDA Margin (teal columns #3FA7C3): negative columns below the baseline labelled (71%), (65%), (45%), (20%) for 2020A Q4, 2021E Q1, 2021E Q2, 2021E Q3; positive columns above the baseline labelled 1% (2021E Q4), 4% (2022E Q1), 8% (2022E Q2), 12% (2022E Q3), 17% (2022E Q4), 23% (2023E), 29% (2024E). Series 2, Ending Physical Occupancy (brown dots #A8854F) labelled 46%, 47%, 53%, 61%, 70%, 73%, 76%, 78%, 81%, 84%, 85%. Series 3, Ending Total Occupancy incl. All Access (dark teal dots #0B7A9A) labelled 47%(2), 49%, 56%, 64%, 75%, 79%, 83%, 86%, 90%, 93%, 95%; each dot sits above its brown dot. Legend across the top at the subtitle level: teal square 'Adj. EBITDA Margin', brown dot 'Ending Physical Occupancy', dark teal dot 'Ending Total Occupancy (incl. All Access)'. Highlight: a teal up-pointing arrow from the annotation to the 2021E Q4 column, with the callout '~70%' in bold teal above the text 'Physical occupancy to achieve Adj. EBITDA Breakeven'. Dashed grey vertical divider at about 79% of width. Dots and labels are placed on a common vertical scale; the 2024E column reaches the top of the chart area.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 29

**Text**

Contents / 01 / Introduction / 02 / WeWork & the Future of Work / 03 / Financial & Transaction Overview / 04 / Appendix / 28 / wework / © 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Two-column split: a full-bleed photograph fills the left 38.2% of the slide edge to edge, and a white right column holds the title and a numbered four-item contents list starting at x 50%. The title sits top-left of the text column at about y 10.5%, the list runs down the column from y 22.5% to 45%, and the footer is anchored bottom-right with a right margin of about 2.5%, holding the page number, a divider, the wordmark logo and the copyright line. The list is left-aligned on a shared indent, with each number set in a fixed column at x 50% and its label at x 53.9%.

**Visuals**

One photograph: an interior office scene at the left edge, full-bleed, cropped to a tall rectangle covering 0 to 38.2% of the slide width and the full slide height. Subject: a long white-topped plywood meeting table with orange-yellow tubular trestle legs, navy upholstered cantilever chairs with chrome frames, a light grey sofa with a black and white patterned cushion and a striped throw at left, potted green plants, a cream ceramic planter with a geometric pattern on the table, a cork stool and a patterned rug on the concrete floor. Behind the table a large window shows a pale hazy sea horizon and green trees. A long brushed-metal pendant tube light hangs diagonally from the top edge. Lighting is soft natural daylight from the window. Dominant colours: cream and white walls, pale grey sea, warm orange-yellow legs, navy chairs, green plants and a multicoloured rug. No overlay or tint; the photo sits flat with no border. Thirteen additional 2 by 2 pixel placeholder images are embedded in the file at positions between x 50% and 90% of the slide; they render as no visible content. The wordmark 'wework' in the footer is a logo: a bold black high-contrast serif lowercase wordmark. No charts, tables or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the full slide, with the photograph covering the left 38.2%. Plain white background with a single thin-free layout; the only colour accents are the teal used for the active contents item and the dark title. Title 'Contents' is set in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham-like) at medium weight, black #000000, about 5% of slide height. Contents items are the same family at medium weight, about 3.9% of slide height. Inactive items are light grey #BBBBBB; the active item 'Financial & Transaction Overview' is teal #3FA7C3. Numbers 01 to 04 are a lighter, widely tracked sans in grey #AAAAAA, about 2.8% of slide height. Footer page number '28' is a regular sans in #222222 about 2% of slide height; the divider is a thin vertical bar in #999999. The copyright line is bold sans in #999999 at about 1.2% of slide height. No shapes, rules, shadows, gradients or rounded corners are visible on the slide. A teal #3FA7C3 rectangle sits behind the photo, covering x 0 to 26.4%, and is hidden by the photograph.

**Layout**

Single white slide with a left-aligned title and subtitle across the top at the left margin of about 2.7 percent. A five-row comparison table sits in the middle: row labels at the left, a 2020A column centred near 51 percent of the width, a centred arrow column between about 63 and 71 percent, and a 2024E column centred near 82 percent, with two thin grey rules under the column headers. Footnotes are bottom-left at small size; the page number 29, a separator, the wework wordmark and the copyright line sit bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Five horizontal teal arrows, one per row, each a thin line with a filled triangular arrowhead pointing right, placed between the 2020A and 2024E values and aligned to each row's centre line. Two thin light grey horizontal rules, one under the 2020A header and one under the 2024E header. A wework wordmark logo in black at bottom-right. No charts, photographs or illustrations. The file also contains many 2 by 2 pixel images at placed positions across the slide; they are not visible on the page and are not listed as elements.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.
