### Slide 31

**Text**

Overview of workstations and memberships / Total Workstations ('000s) / Physical Memberships / All Access Memberships / Available Capacity / 855 / 236 / 619 / 1,030 / 554 / 476 / 954 / 237 / 672 / 45 / 1,057 / 111 / 88 / 859 / 1,246 / 84 / 117 / 1,044 / 1,551 / 77 / 160 / 1,314 / 2019A / 2020A / 2021E / 2022E / 2023E / 2024E / Physical Ending Occupancy / 72% / 46% / 70% / 81% / 84% / 85% / Total Ending Occupancy / 72% / 47%(1) / 75% / 90% / 93% / 95% / Source: Management Projections. / Note: KPIs include IndiaCo and ChinaCo. Excludes WeMemberships. Please see the Definitions page in the appendix for further detail. / 1. Includes ~1% of occupancy from All Access memberships, representing ~7.3k total memberships in 2020. / 30 | wework / © 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Single-slide white page with the title flush left at the top, a chart block in the upper-middle area spanning nearly the full width, a two-row occupancy table beneath the chart aligned to the bar centres, and footnotes bottom-left with page number and wework logo bottom-right. Margins are about 3% left and right; the page number and logo sit at the bottom-right corner and the copyright line sits below them, right-aligned. No slide number is shown in the sense of the deck index; the printed page number is 30.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart with title 'Total Workstations ('000s)' above a thin grey rule. Six columns for 2019A, 2020A, 2021E, 2022E, 2023E and 2024E, bottom-aligned on a dark grey baseline; no y-axis or gridlines are drawn and the chart has no numeric axis range. Series from bottom to top: Physical Memberships (#0087B1 blue), All Access Memberships (#BEE6EC pale cyan), Available Capacity (#8E8EA0 grey). Printed values: 2019A physical 619, available 236, total 855. 2020A physical 476, available 554, total 1,030. 2021E physical 672, All Access 45 (label printed outside the column to the right in blue), available 237, total 954. 2022E physical 859, All Access 88, available 111, total 1,057. 2023E physical 1,044, All Access 117, available 84, total 1,246. 2024E physical 1,314, All Access 160, available 77 (printed outside to the right), total 1,551. Totals are printed in bold dark text above each column. Segment values are printed inside segments in white on blue and grey, and in blue on the pale cyan segments. Legend sits top-left above the columns with three square swatches stacked vertically. No highlighted points. Below the chart are two tables: Physical Ending Occupancy row with values 72%, 46%, 70%, 81%, 84%, 85%; Total Ending Occupancy row with values 72%, 47%(1), 75%, 90%, 93%, 95%. Column centres align with the year labels.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide. Title in near-black sans-serif (geometric grotesque such as Gilroy or similar), regular weight, about 5.5% of slide height. Chart title in dark grey (#222222) medium-weight sans-serif about 2.8% of height. Chart values in regular sans-serif about 2.3% of height, white inside blue and grey, blue #0087B1 on pale cyan. Totals bold near-black #111111 about 2.4%. Section headers 'Physical Ending Occupancy' and 'Total Ending Occupancy' in bold #0087B1 about 2.2%. Occupancy values in regular #444444 about 2.2%. Footnotes in dark grey #333333 regular about 1.5%. Thin light grey rules (#BBBBBB, about 0.1% height) under the chart title, under each occupancy header, and a dark grey baseline (#333333) under the columns. Columns are square-cornered, no shadows, no gradients. Wework wordmark in black bold serif-like logotype.

**Layout**

Two equal-width chart panels side by side in a two-column grid, each with a bold heading and a thin grey rule beneath it, with a dotted-rule metric row under each chart. The slide title sits top-left across the full width at about 6% from the top, left-aligned to a margin of about 3% of the slide width. Footnotes are left-aligned in a block at the bottom left, and the page number, wework logo and copyright line sit bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two bar charts plus two metric rows. Left chart: column chart titled Revenue (Excluding ChinaCo), units $ in billions, six columns with value labels printed above each bar: 2019A $3.2, 2020A $3.2, 2021E $3.2, 2022E $4.6, 2023E $5.8, 2024E $7.0. Columns are flat medium grey-violet #8E8EA0 with no gridlines, no y-axis, and a single dark baseline under the columns; the 2019A to 2021E columns are equal height and the 2022E to 2024E columns step up. No legend; no highlighted points. Right chart: column chart titled Adj. EBITDA (Excluding ChinaCo), units $ in billions, with a horizontal black zero line across the plot. Columns are teal #67B8C2; values printed beside each column: 2019A ($1.9) below the zero line, 2020A ($1.8) below, 2021E ($0.9) below, 2022E $0.5 above, 2023E $1.4 above, 2024E $2.0 above. Negative value labels are in dark teal #2E7D88; positive labels in dark teal #2E7D88 as well. No legend and no y-axis. Below the left chart a metric row titled Core Leased as % of Company Revenue (Excluding ChinaCo)(1) with values 86%, 90%, 90%, 91%, 89%, 86% aligned under the years 2019A to 2024E. Below the right chart a row titled % Margin with values (60%), (55%), (28%), 11%, 23%, 29%. Metric values are in teal #2B9FB0 and are separated from headings by light dotted teal rules. No photographs, icons or illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate financial slide. Title in near-black charcoal #23262E, medium-weight geometric sans-serif (close to Gotham Medium or Graphik), about 5.7% of slide height. Chart headings in charcoal #23262E, semibold geometric sans, about 2.3% of slide height; unit subtitles in regular weight about 1.7%. Chart value labels and axis year labels are medium-weight sans in charcoal #3A3D45 at about 2.0% (the grey value labels) and dark #1E1E1E for years. Negative and positive EBITDA labels are teal. Body footnotes are regular sans in charcoal #222222 at about 1.4% of slide height. Thin grey #B9B9B9 rules under chart headings, dotted teal #7CC4CC rules framing the metric rows. Bars have square corners, no shadows or gradients. Copyright line in regular sans #999999 at 1.23%.

### Slide 33

**Review notes**

- Review: Most main-body slides pair a headline claim with a supporting chart, as on slides 15, 24, 28 and 33, and the template, palette and footer are consistent.

**Text**

Revenue composition / WeWork is a global flexible workspace leader, accessing major geographies with diverse product offerings / Revenue By Geography (Excluding ChinaCo) / ($ in billions) / LatAm / Pacific / Japan / Other / EMEA / US and Canada / $7.0 / $5.8 / $4.6 / $3.2 / $3.2 / $3.2 / 2019A / 2020A / 2021E / 2022E / 2023E / 2024E / Revenue By Product (Excluding ChinaCo) / ($ in billions) / Ventures / Platform / Marketplace / All Access / Core Leased / $7.0 / $5.8 / $4.6 / $3.2 / $3.2 / $3.2 / 2019A / 2020A / 2021E / 2022E / 2023E / 2024E / Source: Management Projections. / Note: Regional revenues only include revenues associated with the core leasing business. "Other" within revenue by geography includes All Access, Marketplace, Platform and Ventures; 2019 and 2020 other revenue was primarily concentrated in US & Canada. / 32 / wework / © 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Two equal columns of charts sit side by side below a full-width left-aligned title and subtitle; the title starts at the left margin (about 2.7% of width) at the top (about 7.6% of height). Each column has a bold-weight section heading with a thin grey rule beneath it, a small units line below, a legend in the upper-left of the plot and bar totals above each bar. A source and note block sits bottom-left, with the page number, wordmark and confidentiality line bottom-right; there are no slide-level margins or boxes beyond the white field.

**Visuals**

Two stacked column charts, both with the same six categories on the horizontal axis: 2019A, 2020A, 2021E, 2022E, 2023E, 2024E. Vertical axis is in dollars billions ('$ in billions') with no tick labels or gridlines printed; only the bar totals are labelled. Left chart: title 'Revenue By Geography (Excluding ChinaCo)'; totals $3.2 (2019A), $3.2 (2020A), $3.2 (2021E), $4.6 (2022E), $5.8 (2023E), $7.0 (2024E); segments from bottom to top are US and Canada (#4AB9CC), EMEA (#0087B1), Other (#256576), Japan (#18383C), Pacific (#8E8EA0), LatAm (#006178); the segment values are not printed, so about values: 2024E US and Canada about $2.9B, EMEA about $2.2B, Other about $1.4B, Japan about $1.1B, Pacific about $0.9B, LatAm about $0.5B; the axis baseline runs under all bars; legend in two columns at upper left (LatAm, Japan, EMEA in the left column; Pacific, Other, US and Canada in the right column). Right chart: title 'Revenue By Product (Excluding ChinaCo)'; totals $3.2, $3.2, $3.2, $4.6, $5.8, $7.0 for the same years; segments from bottom to top are Core Leased (#007394), All Access (#3FA7C3), Marketplace (#5B5B6B), Platform (#C7C7CF), Ventures (#18383C) with the Ventures segment at the top of 2019A-2021E bars; about values: 2024E Core Leased about $6.0B, All Access about $0.5B, Marketplace about $0.3B, Platform about $0.2B; legend in two columns at upper left (Ventures, Marketplace, Core Leased in the left column; Platform, All Access in the right column). No highlighted points. Bars are flat-filled rectangles with no outlines, no data labels inside segments.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide with no decorative elements. Title in a regular-weight geometric sans-serif (close to Gotham-style grotesque), dark charcoal #2A2A33, large, approximately 6.2% of slide height. Subtitle in the same family, regular weight, #2A2A33, about 2.5% of slide height. Section headings in a medium-weight sans, #2A2A33, about 2.4% of slide height, each underlined by a hairline rule #BFBFC4 about 0.1% high. Units lines and legend labels in regular sans, #2A2A33, about 1.6 to 2.0% of slide height. Totals in medium-weight sans, #2A2A33. Chart palette: teal and slate family (#4AB9CC, #0087B1, #256576, #18383C, #8E8EA0, #006178, #007394, #3FA7C3, #5B5B6B, #C7C7CF). Source and note text in regular sans about 1.4% of slide height, #2A2A33. Copyright line bold sans, #999999. Wordmark in a black bold serif lowercase.

© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Two-panel layout: a wide left panel (about 0 to 42% of width) holding a waterfall chart, a thin vertical divider line at about x 44.5% running from y 24% to y 85%, and a right panel (about 46% to 96%) holding a data table above a column chart. The title sits top-left at about x 2.7%, y 6% with the subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on a left margin of about 2.7%. Each panel has a bold-ish heading with superscript footnote markers and a thin grey rule beneath it. Footnotes are left-aligned in the bottom band from y 88% to 97%; the page number '33', a vertical bar and the wework wordmark sit bottom-right at about y 93%, with the copyright line beneath them, right-aligned to about x 98%.

**Visuals**

Left: a floating-bar waterfall chart titled '2019 Consolidated Mature Building Margin' with units '($ in billions)' printed above the plot. It has no visible y-axis or gridlines; bars are scaled as percentages of the first bar (100% = full height, plot base at about y 75% of slide). Four bars left to right: 'Membership & service revenue' a solid light-teal bar of full height labelled 100% inside it in dark text; 'Rent and tenancy expenses' a pale-blue floating bar labelled 54% inside, spanning from the top of the plot down to about 54% of full height; 'Other building expenses' a pale-blue floating bar labelled 19% inside, sitting between about 19% and 38% of full height; 'Building margin(1)' a solid deep-blue bar labelled 27% inside in white, rising from the base to about 27% of full height, highlighted as the result. A dotted grey horizontal reference line runs at the top of the Building margin bar across the right chart area from about x 35% to 96%. Right: a table titled 'Supplemental Metrics (2019)' in blue, with three rows and seven value columns, columns in order New York, London, Tokyo, San Francisco, Boston, Top 6-20 markets, Consolidated Mature Locations (column headings not printed in the table, order taken from the bar chart beneath). Row 1 'Mature Physical Occupancy Rate': 92%, 90%, 95%, 91%, 89%, 85%, 88%. Row 2 'Mature market revenue % of total': 14%, 9%, 2%, 4%, 2%, 16%, N/A. Row 3 'Total market revenue % of total': 18%, 15%, 5%, 5%, 3%, 26%, N/A. Thin grey horizontal rules separate rows. Below the table is a vertical column chart of building margin by market with no axis or gridlines, columns in order New York 25%, London 30%, Tokyo 41%, San Francisco 35%, Boston 38%, Top 6-20 markets 21%, Consolidated Mature Locations 27%, each value printed above its column. Columns are pale teal #92D5E0 except the last column which is deep blue #0087B1 and highlighted. The same dotted grey reference line at 27% runs across the chart. Column heights are proportional to the values, all sitting on a dark grey baseline at about y 75%. No legend. No logos except the wework wordmark in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Titles and headline text are dark charcoal #2B2D33 in a geometric humanist sans-serif (similar to Gilroy or Avenir), regular weight, title about 5.5% of slide height (capitals about 3.9%). Subtitle is the same charcoal sans-serif, regular weight, about 2.5% of slide height. Panel headings are charcoal sans-serif, medium weight, about 2.6% of height. The 'Supplemental Metrics (2019)' label is bold-ish blue #0087B1 sans-serif, about 2.3% of height. Table and chart labels are charcoal #2B2D33 sans-serif, regular, about 2.2% of height; footnotes are about 1.2% of height in charcoal. Accent colours: deep blue #0087B1 for highlighted bars and the blue label; pale teal #92D5E0 and very pale blue #D3EEF3 for the other bars. Hairline rules are grey #8A8F98 about 0.1% of height thick; the vertical divider is a grey-violet #8E8FA6 line about 0.2% wide. The dotted reference lines are grey #8A8F98 with round dots. No corner radii, shadows or gradients. Bars are flat rectangles with square corners.

**Layout**

Single full-width content slide with a left-aligned title and subtitle across the top, one column-based bar chart in the upper-middle band over a table of rows aligned to the same nine period columns, footnotes at the bottom-left, and a page number and wordmark at bottom-right. Title and subtitle sit at the left margin of about 2.8% of width, with a white background and no visible frame.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart titled Total Liquidity(1) with subtitle ($ in billions). Nine bars in dark teal #00556A with no gridlines and no y-axis. Bars left to right: 2019A value $3.4 (footnote 2 superscript), 2020A $2.9, Q1 2021E $2.2, Q2 2021E $1.8, Q3 2021E $2.3, Q4 2021E $2.3, 2022E $2.1, 2023E $2.5, 2024E $3.4. Values are printed above each bar, so no estimates are needed. The bars are of equal width and share a common baseline with a thin teal horizontal axis line beneath them. Two dashed callout boxes annotate Q3 2021E (issuance text above a short dashed leader line down to the $2.3 label) and 2022E (leader line down to the $2.1 label). A thin dotted grey vertical divider separates the 2021E quarters from 2022E. Below the chart a table has four rows: Total Adj. EBITDA (excl. ChinaCo) with values ($1,943), ($1,754), ($424), ($321), ($171), $8, $485, $1,359, $1,988; Negative impact of non-cash GAAP straight-line lease cost with values ($832), ($554), ($108), ($71), ($59), ($47), ($101), ($80), ($61); Positive impact of amortization on lease incentives with values $234, $340, $78, $73, $72, $72, $288, $296, $306; Net Capital Expenditures with values ($2,183), ($49), $16, $26, ($1), ($13), ($301), ($487), ($577). Rows 1 and 4 have pale mint #E9F7F9 bands. The table columns match the nine bar columns. Negative values use parentheses. Row labels are left-aligned and the numbers right-aligned.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title is near-black #1F1F1F in a regular-weight geometric humanist sans-serif (Gill Sans-like), around 6 percent of slide height. Subtitle is near-black #1F1F1F in the same family, regular weight, around 2.7 percent. Chart labels and the Total Liquidity heading are teal #0087B1 in the same sans. Bars and axis line are teal #00556A. Bar value labels are teal #0087B1, about 2.6 percent of slide height. Table text is dark grey #1F1F1F, about 1.9 percent; the two EBITDA and capex rows are regular weight and the lease-adjustment rows are italic. Callout boxes are white #FFFFFF with 1 px dashed teal #00556A borders and dark grey text about 1.6 percent. Footnotes are dark grey #333333 about 1.25 percent. Page number is dark grey #333333. Line shapes are thin: 1 px dashed teal leaders, 1 px dotted grey divider, 1 px grey rule above the table and below it. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

### Slide 36

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The capital structure table is set out as a clean ledger of coupon, maturity and outstanding amounts, so the debt picture reads at a glance.
- What would change the score: I would shorten the logo-heavy and text-heavy pages (6, 9, 10) and pull the key capital structure rows on slide 36 into a clearer hierarchy.

**Layout**

Single-slide table layout on a white field: the title sits top-left at about 2.7% from the left edge with a thin teal rule beneath it spanning the full content width (about 3.4% to 96.6%). A four-column financial table follows (label column, then Coupon, Maturity and Outstanding columns, with Outstanding right-aligned to about 96.6%). Coloured total rows span the full width as bands. Footnotes are left-aligned at about 2.2% from the left in the lower area, with the page number and wework logo bottom-right and the copyright line at the very bottom right.

**Visuals**

Table with four columns: row labels; Coupon; Maturity; Outstanding. Header row: '($ in millions as of 6/30/2021E)', 'Coupon', 'Maturity', 'Outstanding'. Rows: Cash and Cash Equivalents(1) – blank, blank, $665. $1.75bn LC Balance (Wholly Owned)(2) – 5.600%, 2/10/2023, 1,274. $1.1bn Senior Secured SoftBank Notes(3) – 12.500%, –, –. $46mm LC Balance (Japan) – 0.200%, NA, 8. Subsidiary Debt – 2.500% – 3.000%, Various, 43. Total Secured Debt (light teal band) – $1,325. Net Secured Debt (light teal band) – $660. Senior Notes – 7.875%, 5/1/2025, 669. SoftBank Senior Unsecured Notes – 5.000%, 7/10/2025, 2,200. Total Debt (light teal band) – $4,194. Net Debt (light teal band) – $3,529. Total Funded Debt(4) (dark teal band) – $2,912. Total Net Funded Debt(4) (dark teal band) – $2,247. Memo rows: Total Available Global Cash(5) – $667; Undrawn Secured Financing Commitments(3) – $1,100; Undrawn Unsecured Financing Commitments – –; Total Undrawn Financing Commitments + Global Cash(3) – $1,767. Thin teal rule (#1A8FA8, about 0.2% of height) sits under the header row. No photographs, charts or icons; the wework wordmark in dark grey serif is the only logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title in dark charcoal #2B2F36, regular-weight geometric sans-serif similar to Gotham, about 5.5% of slide height. Body table text in the same family, light-to-regular weight, about 2.4% of slide height, colour #2B2F36. Light teal bands #92D5E0 for secured, net and total debt rows; dark teal bands #007394 for total funded and net funded debt rows, with white text on the dark bands. Accent rule teal #1A8FA8. Footnotes in the same sans at about 1.2% of height, colour #2B2F36. Page number '35' in regular sans, colour #2B2F36. Copyright line in grey #999999 at about 1.2% of height. Bands are flat with square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

Title left margin about 2.7% of width, top about 6% of height. Footnotes sit at the bottom-left margin; page number and wework logo sit bottom-right inside the navy panel with the copyright line beneath. Slide number 36 is printed in the footer.

**Visuals**

Pie chart titled 'Pro forma ownership (1)' with no axes or legend; four wedges with labels printed outside the pie. Wedge values: BowX Public Shares 6.1% (light aqua #92D5E0, top wedge starting at 12 o'clock and running clockwise); PIPE Shares 10.1% (mid aqua #3FA7C3); BowX Founder Shares 1.1% (bright blue #0087B1, thin sliver); Existing WeWork Rollover Equity 82.7% (dark teal #007394, the large remainder). Wedges are separated by thin white radial lines. Pie is centred near x 22.5%, y 62%, diameter about 47% of slide height. No highlighted points. Right side has no imagery; no photographs, icons or logos besides the small wework wordmark in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Split background: white #FFFFFF on the left and solid navy #004359 panel on the right (from x 58.9% to 100%). Clean corporate style. Title size about 5.8% of slide height, subtitle and right heading about 4% of height, body bullets about 3% of height, footnotes about 1.4%. No shadows, gradients or corner radii.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white page: a left column titled Pro forma valuation (about x 3 to 52%) and a right column titled Sources & Uses (about x 53 to 93%), both under a single left-aligned title at top-left with a left margin of about 3%. Two bullet callouts sit below the left table, the footnote block sits bottom-left, and the page number 37, a vertical bar and the wework wordmark sit bottom-right above the copyright line.

**Visuals**

Two tables and two bullet callouts, no photographs or charts. Left table 'Pro forma valuation' has no column headers and two value rows: Illustrative WeWork Share Price $10.00; Pro Forma Shares Outstanding 792.7; Pro Forma Equity Value $7,927 (highlighted row); (+) Pro Forma Debt(1) 2,912; (-) Pro Forma Cash(1) (1,873); WeWork Enterprise Value $8,966 (highlighted row). Right table 'Sources & Uses' has a grey Sources header row, then Existing BowX Cash in Trust(2) $483, Expected PIPE Proceeds 800, WeWork Rollover Equity 6,553, Existing WeWork Cash(3) 665, and a highlighted Total Sources $8,501 row; then a grey Uses header row, Cash to Pro Forma 6/30/2021 Balance Sheet $1,873, Equity Consideration to WeWork Shareholders 6,553, Estimated Fees & Expenses 75, and a highlighted Total Uses $8,501 row. **Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradients, shadows or imagery. Titles and section headings are teal #1B8AAD for the section headings and charcoal #2B2B33 for the slide title. Body and table text is charcoal #2B2B33. **Visuals**

Photograph: an aerial, high-angle view of an atrium lounge, shown in a full-height rectangle on the left. Framing: diagonal top-down view across a curved black banquette with tan wood edges, planted green hedges in raised wooden planters, and a white tufted-rug coffee table. A glass ceiling is supported by white steel trusses and tubular columns. Several people are seated or walking: one at a banquette with a tablet, one at a table with a laptop, one in a plaid shirt on the lower banquette, and others on the floor. Dominant colours are warm tan wood, black upholstery, white, and green foliage. Lighting is soft daylight through the glass roof, with dappled shadows on the light wood floor. The image is cropped to a hard vertical edge at x 38.1% and is not boxed. There is no overlay or tint. Other visuals: the wordmark 'wework' in a black high-contrast serif-style logotype at the bottom right. Thin structural lines: none.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title and body text in near-black charcoal #2B2B33, with teal #3DA8C4 for actual-year headers and dark teal #163B3A for estimate-year headers; total rows shaded #D8EEF3. Typeface is a geometric humanist sans-serif, close to Gilroy or Circular, in regular weight for title and body, bold for section labels, column headers and total rows, italic for growth rows. Title is about 6% of slide height, body table text about 2.2% and footnotes about 1.2%. Flat design with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners; separators are thin hairlines.

### Slide 41

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are real, though: slide 43's waterfall is hard to follow, with a mislabeled step order and a callout bracket that is unclear, slides 41, 42 and 47 to 50 are dense unreadable tables, and the disclaimer pages 2 and 3 are walls of tiny text.
- Suggested repair: Simplify the slide 43 waterfall with clearer step order and labeling, and cut the dense financial tables on slides 41, 42 and 47 to 50 to readable size or split them across pages.

### Slide 42

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are real, though: slide 43's waterfall is hard to follow, with a mislabeled step order and a callout bracket that is unclear, slides 41, 42 and 47 to 50 are dense unreadable tables, and the disclaimer pages 2 and 3 are walls of tiny text.

Left margin is about 2.5% of slide width and the top margin about 0.5%.

none. The slide contains only a text table and footnote text; no chart, photograph, icon or logo graphic apart from the small wework wordmark at bottom right. ### Slide 43

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A waterfall-style bridge walks from revenue to Adj. EBITDA on the existing core portfolio, showing how the 2023 core business case is built step by step.
- Review: The weaknesses are real, though: slide 43's waterfall is hard to follow, with a mislabeled step order and a callout bracket that is unclear, slides 41, 42 and 47 to 50 are dense unreadable tables, and the disclaimer pages 2 and 3 are walls of tiny text.
- Suggested repair: Simplify the slide 43 waterfall with clearer step order and labeling, and cut the dense financial tables on slides 41, 42 and 47 to 50 to readable size or split them across pages.

**Text**

WeWork Core at maturity achieves ~$5bn of Revenue and ~$1bn of Adj. EBITDA / ($ in millions) / $5,801 / ($3,484) / ($112) / ($846) / $1,359 / ($400) / ($225) / $307 / $1,042 / 2023E Revenue (excl. Ventures) / 2023E Adj. Rent, Tenancy, and Other Building Expenses(1) / 2023E Adj. Pre-Opening Expenses(1) / 2023E Adj. Functional Expenses(1) / 2023E Total Adj. EBITDA / 2023E All Access & Platform Revenue / 2023E Core Leased Incremental Growth Revenue / 2023E Core Leased Incremental Growth Expenses(2) / 2023E Adj. EBITDA on Existing Core Leased Portfolio / 2023E Revenue (excl. Ventures) less All Access, Platform and incremental Core Leased: $5,176mm / 1 percentage point of 2023E core occupancy results in ~$59mm of revenue.(3) / Source: Management projections. / Note: The existing Core Leased base plan includes additional growth from currently contracted leases. The above analysis excludes Ventures. / 1. Adj. Rent, Tenancy, and Other Building Expenses, Adj. Pre-Opening Expenses, and Adj. Functional Expenses exclude Ventures expenses, which are all reflected in Ventures EBITDA. / 2. Expenses reflect Adj. Rent, Tenancy and Other Building Expenses, Adj. Pre-Opening Expenses, and Adj. Functional Expenses associated with Core Leased Incremental Growth Revenue. / 3. Assumes 857k existing Core Leased ending workstations and $573 Core Leased ARPM. / 42 / wework / © 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Single full-width slide with the title across the top left at the margin, the subtitle ($ in millions) under it, and a waterfall chart occupying the middle band with a dashed-outline callout box in the upper right of the chart area. A light-blue takeaway band spans the lower part above the footnotes, and the footnotes sit bottom left with the page number, WeWork wordmark and copyright bottom right. Text is left aligned at about 3% margin; chart category labels are centred under each bar on a shared baseline.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical waterfall bar chart titled by the slide headline, units $ in millions, no visible y-axis or gridlines, shared horizontal baseline at about 66.6% of slide height. Series, left to right, with printed labels: 2023E Revenue (excl. Ventures) $5,801 teal total bar full height; 2023E Adj. Rent, Tenancy, and Other Building Expenses ($3,484) grey floating bar from top of first bar down; 2023E Adj. Pre-Opening Expenses ($112) thin grey floating bar; 2023E Adj. Functional Expenses ($846) grey floating bar; 2023E Total Adj. EBITDA $1,359 teal total bar; 2023E All Access & Platform Revenue ($400) grey floating bar; 2023E Core Leased Incremental Growth Revenue ($225) thin grey floating bar; 2023E Core Leased Incremental Growth Expenses $307 dark blue positive bar with white label; 2023E Adj. EBITDA on Existing Core Leased Portfolio $1,042 teal total bar. Bars are flat, no outlines. Callout: dashed dark-teal rectangle reading 2023E Revenue (excl. Ventures) less All Access, Platform and incremental Core Leased: $5,176mm, with a dashed connector line from the top of the $5,801 bar bracket to the callout and a solid bracket spanning the All Access and Core Leased Incremental Growth Revenue bars with a dashed drop line to the callout. No legend, no highlighted data points beyond the callout. Logo: WeWork wordmark bottom right in black.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Titles black #000000 in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham or Graphik-like) medium weight, title cap height about 4.4% of slide height, em about 5.0. Body and labels dark #1A1A1A, regular weight, about 1.6 to 2.0 percent em. Chart totals teal #3FA7C3, grey floating bars #E8E8EC, dark positive bar #0E86AE, negative value labels in blue #1A7FA8, takeaway band fill #D3EEF3 with black text. Callout dashed outline dark teal #1B4F5E, brackets dark teal #1B4F5E, baseline #222222 thin. Footnotes in dark grey #333333 regular sans, about 1.2 em. Copyright grey #999999. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners; all shapes square.

### Slide 45

**Text**

Terms & Definitions
Overall Business Definitions:
● Core Leased (Core WeWork): WeWork’s existing flexible workspace business, including incremental growth for WeWork’s flexible workspace business.
● Marketplace: provides a holistic marketplace ecosystem in which members can access value-added services and flexible real estate products. Enables members to create a custom workplace experience that meets their business needs. Many of these digitized space and service offerings are offered as a workplace solution via the WeWork Member app.
● All Access: monthly membership providing an individual with access to any WeWork location.
● Platform: represents WeWork buildings whereby the Company enters into asset-light management or franchise agreements with landlords and operates the space in exchange for a fee.
● Ventures: includes WeWork Capital Advisors and expected to also include WeLive from 2022E.
● Small and Medium Businesses (SMBs): 1-99 full time employees (FTEs).
● Medium and Large Businesses (MLBs): 100-499 FTEs.
● Enterprise: 500+ FTEs.
● Mature: refers to locations that are open for longer than 12 months.
● Non-mature: refers to locations that are open for less than 12 months.
● ChinaCo: no ChinaCo management fee assumed over the projection period given terms of the agreement whereby the Company will be entitled to a management fee beginning on the later of 2022 or the first fiscal year after October 2020 in which EBIT of ChinaCo is positive. For reference, ChinaCo was deconsolidated in October 2020, and both ChinaCo and IndiaCo are unconsolidated equity method investments.
● WeMembership: Legacy product that was a virtual membership which provides user login access to the WeWork member network online or through the mobile application as well as access to service offerings and the right to reserve space on an à la carte basis, among other benefits. Each WeMembership is considered to be one Membership.
Operating KPIs:
● Workstations: represents the estimated number of workstations available at open locations.
● Memberships: are the cumulative number of WeWork physical and All Access memberships.
● Physical Occupancy: is the number of physical memberships divided by total workstations.
● Total Occupancy: is the number of physical memberships plus All Access memberships divided by of total workstations.
● Total Enterprise Membership: represents memberships attributable to enterprise members, which are organizations with greater than 500 FTEs. Enterprise membership percentage represents the percentage of our memberships attributable to these organizations.
44 | wework
© 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

### Slide 46

**Text**

Terms & Definitions (Cont’d) / Financial Metrics: / ● Building Margin: excludes building related depreciation and amortization which would otherwise be included in gross profit determined in accordance with US GAAP; In addition, Building Margin as calculated above for our Mature Locations only includes the direct location operating expenses incurred in operating those locations as the Company does not allocate certain community support expenses that are necessary to operate our buildings but not directly tied to an individual building. Building Margin also excludes broker fees and marketing expenses, as well as stock-based compensation expense paid to community employees as such costs are non-cash in nature and not allocated to individual buildings. / ● Adj. EBITDA: is a non-GAAP measure that we define as net loss before income tax (benefit) provision, interest and other (income)expense, depreciation and amortization expense, stock-based compensation expense, expense related to stock-based payments for services rendered by consultants, income or expense relating to the changes in fair value of assets and liabilities remeasured to fair value on a recurring basis, expense related to costs associated with mergers, acquisitions, divestitures and capital raising activities, legal, tax and regulatory reserves or settlements, significant non-ordinary course asset impairment charges and, to the extent applicable, any impact of discontinued operations, restructuring charges, and other gains and losses on operating assets. Refer to page 45 for a reconciliation of Net Income to Adj. EBITDA. / ○ Adj. EBITDA Margin: Adj. EBITDA divided by Total Revenue. / ● Adj. Rent, Tenancy and Other Building Expenses: consists of location operating expenses, such as rent contractually paid or payable, including the impact of non-cash GAAP straight-line lease cost and the amortization of lease incentives, real estate and related taxes and common area maintenance charges and other building opex, such as utilities, maintenance and cleaning, insurance, food, and other consumables. Excludes depreciation and amortization expense, stock-based compensation expense, and certain community support expenses that are necessary to operate our buildings but not directly tied to an individual building. / ● Adj. Pre-Opening Expense: consist of expenses (including all lease costs, which also include non-cash GAAP straight-line lease cost) incurred before a location opens for member operations. Excludes depreciation and amortization expense and stock-based compensation expense. / ● Adj. Functional Expense: consist of sales and marketing, general and administrative and sourcing, development and other expenses, and certain community support expenses that are necessary to operate our buildings but not directly tied to an individual building. Excludes depreciation and amortization expense, stock-based compensation expense, expense related to stock-based payments for services rendered by consultants, expense related to costs associated with mergers, acquisitions divestitures, and capital raising activities, legal, tax, and regulatory reserves or settlements, and legal expenses related to regulatory investigations and litigations arising from the 2019 cancelled IPO. / ● Non-Cash GAAP Straight-Line Lease Costs: are required under GAAP to recognize the impact of “free rent” periods and lease cost escalation clauses on a straight-line basis over the terms of our leases. / ● Amortization of Lease Incentives: the amortization of cash received for tenant improvement allowances and broker commissions (collectively “lease incentives”), amortized on a straight-line basis over the terms of our leases as a reduction to the total operating lease cost. / 45 / wework / © 2021 WeWork. Confidential.

**Layout**

Single left-aligned text column with a 2.7% left margin, title at top-left (starting about 6% from the top) and the body text block running from about 14.5% to 89% of the height. Bullets sit at about 4.6% and text wraps at about 94% of the width. The page number 45 and the wework logo sit bottom-right at about 95% height, with the copyright line directly below them at 96.8%; there is no slide-number field beyond the printed 45.

**Visuals**

No photographs, charts, tables or icons. The only non-text element is the WeWork wordmark logo at bottom right: the lowercase word wework in a dark charcoal bold serif-like typeface, about 9.5% wide and 2% high.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradients, shadows or decorative shapes. Title in dark charcoal #2B2B33 in a geometric sans-serif (Gilroy-like) medium weight at about 5.4% of slide height. Body text in the same charcoal #2B2B33 in a geometric sans-serif (Avenir/Gilroy-like) regular weight at about 2.0% of slide height with generous line spacing of about 3.3% of height between wrapped lines. Bolded lead-in labels such as Building Margin and Adj. EBITDA are in teal-blue #1A8FB8 with a colon in charcoal. Bullets are solid black round dots at level one and hollow circles at level two, indented about 4.6% from the left edge with text at about 11.8%. The copyright line is small grey #999999 bold sans-serif.

### Slide 47

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are real, though: slide 43's waterfall is hard to follow, with a mislabeled step order and a callout bracket that is unclear, slides 41, 42 and 47 to 50 are dense unreadable tables, and the disclaimer pages 2 and 3 are walls of tiny text.
- Suggested repair: Simplify the slide 43 waterfall with clearer step order and labeling, and cut the dense financial tables on slides 41, 42 and 47 to 50 to readable size or split them across pages.

**Layout**

Single full-width slide with the title at top left, starting at about 2.7% from the left edge, and a subtitle block below it. **Design**

China) numeric columns, and a boxed 'Mature & Non-Mature Split' group of three columns on the right. Numbered footnotes sit bottom-left in small type; page number '47', a vertical divider and the wework wordmark sit bottom-right with the confidentiality line beneath, right-aligned.

One table with 7 numeric columns and about 22 rows. Columns: row label; Total Consolidated · All Locations; China; Total (excl. China)(4); Mature; Non-Mature; Other(4). Horizontal black rules sit under the column headers, above Location operating expenses, above Location gross profit, and above the reconciliation line. Also one wework wordmark logo in black lowercase bottom-right. No charts or photographs.

### Slide 50

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are real, though: slide 43's waterfall is hard to follow, with a mislabeled step order and a callout bracket that is unclear, slides 41, 42 and 47 to 50 are dense unreadable tables, and the disclaimer pages 2 and 3 are walls of tiny text.
- Suggested repair: Simplify the slide 43 waterfall with clearer step order and labeling, and cut the dense financial tables on slides 41, 42 and 47 to 50 to readable size or split them across pages.

**Text**

FY19 and FY20 Free Cash Flow reconciliation (GAAP to Non-GAAP)
Free Cash Flow
($ in millions)
• Free Cash Flow is defined as cash flow from operating activities less cash purchases of property and equipment, each as presented in the Company's consolidated statements of cash flows calculated in accordance with GAAP.
• A reconciliation of net cash provided by (used in) operating activities, the most comparable GAAP measure, to Free Cash Flow is set forth below:

**Layout**

Single-column slide with the title across the top at left, a subtitle block ('Free Cash Flow' with '($ in millions)') beneath it, and a thin full-width rule separating the header from the body. Two bullets sit left-aligned under the rule, followed by a left-aligned financial table spanning the lower-middle, with the table's numeric columns right-side aligned at the right margin. The footnote sits bottom-left, and the page number, wework wordmark and copyright line sit bottom-right; margins are about 3% on the left and 5% on the right.

**Visuals**

One table, no chart, photograph, logo graphic or icon apart from the wework wordmark in the footer. Table columns: row label (left), 2020 (right), 2019 (right), with a 'Year Ended December 31,' spanner above the two year columns, underlined by a short dark rule. Rows: 'Net cash provided by (used in) operating activities(1)' $(857) and $(448); 'Less: Cash purchases of property and equipment(1)' (1,441) and (3,488); 'Free Cash Flow' $(2,298) and $(3,936). A dark horizontal rule (#222222, about 0.15% of slide height) sits under the column headers. The Free Cash Flow row is highlighted with a pale blue band (#D9EDF3) spanning the full table width. Values are printed, so no estimates are needed.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, flat and minimal. Title in dark charcoal #2B2D35, set in a geometric sans-serif such as Gotham Regular at about 5.2% of slide height (cap height about 3.6%). Subtitle 'Free Cash Flow' in medium-weight sans at about 2.9% of slide height, with '($ in millions)' in regular sans at about 1.9%. Body bullet text in regular sans-serif, charcoal #2B2B2B, about 2.45% of slide height with generous line spacing. The defined term 'Free Cash Flow' in the first bullet is teal #3AAFCB as the single accent colour. Table text is regular sans in #2B2B2B at about 2.5%. Divider lines are light grey #C8C8D0 under the subtitle and dark #222222 under the table headers. Table highlight band is pale blue #D9EDF3 with no rounding or shadow. Footer text is about 1.2% of slide height; the copyright is light grey #999999 and the wework wordmark is a black serif-style logotype.
